package com.um.clearflow;

public class HazardReport {
    public String id;
    public String title;
    public String description;
    public String locationName;
    public String hazardType;      // "Flash Flood", "Fallen Tree", "Blocked Drain", "Slope / Landslide"
    public String severity;        // "Low", "Medium", "Critical"
    public String status;          // "Submitted", "Verified", "In Progress - JPPHB", "Resolved"
    public double latitude;
    public double longitude;
    public String timestamp;
    public int upvotes;
    public int resolveVotes;       // FR 2.2: 众包"已解除"投票数 (满3票自动变为 Resolved)
    public double aiConfidence;
    public String aiTriageNote;
    public String reporterEmail;   // FR 2.3: 绑定上报人邮箱，支持筛选"我的上报"
    public String timeline;        // FR 2.3: 工单状态流转时间线记录
    public String photoUri;        // FR 2.1: 现场抓拍照片本地持久化路径
    public long createdAt;         // epoch ms; orders the list identically on every device after sync

    public HazardReport(String id, String title, String description, String locationName,
                        String hazardType, String severity, String status,
                        double latitude, double longitude, String timestamp,
                        int upvotes, double aiConfidence, String aiTriageNote) {
        this(id, title, description, locationName, hazardType, severity, status,
                latitude, longitude, timestamp, upvotes, 0, aiConfidence, aiTriageNote,
                "demo.student@siswa.um.edu.my", timestamp + " • Report Submitted", "");
    }

    public HazardReport(String id, String title, String description, String locationName,
                        String hazardType, String severity, String status,
                        double latitude, double longitude, String timestamp,
                        int upvotes, int resolveVotes, double aiConfidence, String aiTriageNote,
                        String reporterEmail, String timeline) {
        this(id, title, description, locationName, hazardType, severity, status,
                latitude, longitude, timestamp, upvotes, resolveVotes, aiConfidence, aiTriageNote,
                reporterEmail, timeline, "");
    }

    public HazardReport(String id, String title, String description, String locationName,
                        String hazardType, String severity, String status,
                        double latitude, double longitude, String timestamp,
                        int upvotes, int resolveVotes, double aiConfidence, String aiTriageNote,
                        String reporterEmail, String timeline, String photoUri) {
        this.id = id;
        this.title = title;
        this.description = description;
        this.locationName = locationName;
        this.hazardType = hazardType;
        this.severity = severity;
        this.status = status;
        this.latitude = latitude;
        this.longitude = longitude;
        this.timestamp = timestamp;
        this.upvotes = upvotes;
        this.resolveVotes = resolveVotes;
        this.aiConfidence = aiConfidence;
        this.aiTriageNote = aiTriageNote;
        this.reporterEmail = reporterEmail != null ? reporterEmail : "anonymous@siswa.um.edu.my";
        this.timeline = timeline != null ? timeline : (timestamp + " • Submitted");
        this.photoUri = photoUri != null ? photoUri : "";
        this.createdAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    // 深拷贝构造函数：防止 UI 线程与后台线程并发修改同一个对象引用
    public HazardReport(HazardReport other) {
        this.id = other.id;
        this.title = other.title;
        this.description = other.description;
        this.locationName = other.locationName;
        this.hazardType = other.hazardType;
        this.severity = other.severity;
        this.status = other.status;
        this.latitude = other.latitude;
        this.longitude = other.longitude;
        this.timestamp = other.timestamp;
        this.upvotes = other.upvotes;
        this.resolveVotes = other.resolveVotes;
        this.aiConfidence = other.aiConfidence;
        this.aiTriageNote = other.aiTriageNote;
        this.reporterEmail = other.reporterEmail;
        this.timeline = other.timeline;
        this.photoUri = other.photoUri != null ? other.photoUri : "";
        this.createdAt = other.createdAt;
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\MainActivity.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import android.Manifest;
import android.annotation.SuppressLint;
import android.app.AlertDialog;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.location.Location;
import android.location.LocationManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.view.View;
import android.widget.Toast;

import java.util.Locale;

import androidx.activity.EdgeToEdge;
import androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher;
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat;
import androidx.core.graphics.Insets;
import androidx.core.location.LocationManagerCompat;
import androidx.core.os.CancellationSignal;
import androidx.core.view.ViewCompat;
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat;
import androidx.core.view.WindowInsetsCompat;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.fragment.app.FragmentManager;
import androidx.fragment.app.FragmentTransaction;

import com.google.android.material.bottomnavigation.BottomNavigationView;

public class MainActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private static final long MAX_SOS_LOCATION_AGE_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000L;
    private static final long SOS_GPS_TIMEOUT_MS = 4000L;

    private Fragment mapFragment;
    private Fragment reportFragment;
    private Fragment weatherAiFragment;
    private Fragment profileFragment;
    private Fragment activeFragment;
    private final Handler mainHandler = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
    private CancellationSignal pendingSosCancellation;
    private Runnable pendingSosTimeout;

    private final Runnable syncTicker = new Runnable() {
        @Override
        public void run() {
            CampusRepository.getInstance().requestSync();
            mainHandler.postDelayed(this, 30_000L);
        }
    };

    @Override
    protected void onStart() {
        super.onStart();
        mainHandler.post(syncTicker);          // sync on launch and then every 30 s while visible
    }

    @Override
    protected void onStop() {
        mainHandler.removeCallbacks(syncTicker);
        super.onStop();
    }

    private final ActivityResultLauncher<String> notifPermLauncher =
            registerForActivityResult(new ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission(), isGranted -> {
                // Notification permission callback
            });
    private final ActivityResultLauncher<String[]> locationPermLauncher =
            registerForActivityResult(new ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions(), result -> {
                boolean ok = Boolean.TRUE.equals(result.get(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION))
                        || Boolean.TRUE.equals(result.get(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION));
                if (ok) {
                    shareLiveLocation();
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(this, "Location permission is required to share GPS.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                }
            });

    public void showSosDialog() {
        CampusRepository repo = CampusRepository.getInstance();
        String emergencyPhone = repo.getProfileField("emergency_phone", "").trim();
        String[] options = {
                "📍 Share GPS Fix with Another App (accuracy and age shown)",
                "🚓 Call UM Security Office (" + EmergencyContacts.UM_SECURITY_DISPLAY + ", 24h)",
                "🏥 Call UM Medical Centre / UMMC (" + EmergencyContacts.UMMC_DISPLAY + ")",
                "🩺 Call UM Clinic (" + EmergencyContacts.UM_CLINIC_DISPLAY + ")",
                "🚑 Call Malaysia Emergency Services (" + EmergencyContacts.NATIONAL_EMERGENCY + ")",
                "📞 Call My Emergency Contact" + (emergencyPhone.isEmpty() ? " (not set)" : " (" + emergencyPhone + ")")
        };
        new AlertDialog.Builder(this)
                .setTitle("🚨 UM-ClearFlow Emergency SOS")
                .setItems(options, (d, which) -> {
                    if (which == 0) shareLiveLocationWithPermission();
                    else if (which == 1) dial(EmergencyContacts.UM_SECURITY_DIAL);
                    else if (which == 2) dial(EmergencyContacts.UMMC_DIAL);
                    else if (which == 3) dial(EmergencyContacts.UM_CLINIC_DIAL);
                    else if (which == 4) dial(EmergencyContacts.NATIONAL_EMERGENCY);
                    else if (emergencyPhone.isEmpty()) {
                        Toast.makeText(this, "Set an emergency contact in the Profile tab first.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    } else dial(emergencyPhone);
                })
                .setNegativeButton("Cancel", null)
                .show();
    }

    private void shareLiveLocationWithPermission() {
        boolean fine = ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)
                == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
        boolean coarse = ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION)
                == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
        if (fine || coarse) {
            shareLiveLocation();
        } else {
            locationPermLauncher.launch(new String[]{
                    Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                    Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION});
        }
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private Location bestLastKnownLocation() {
        LocationManager lm = (LocationManager) getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
        if (lm == null) return null;
        Location best = null;
        for (String p : new String[]{LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER,
                LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER, LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER}) {
            try {
                Location l = lm.getLastKnownLocation(p);
                if (l != null && (best == null || l.getTime() > best.getTime())) best = l;
            } catch (SecurityException | IllegalArgumentException ignored) {}
        }
        return best;
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private void shareLiveLocation() {
        cancelPendingSosLocationRequest();
        final Location cached = bestLastKnownLocation();
        boolean freshEnough = cached != null
                && (System.currentTimeMillis() - cached.getTime() <= MAX_SOS_LOCATION_AGE_MS);
        if (freshEnough) {
            dispatchSosShareIntent(cached);
            return;
        }

        LocationManager lm = (LocationManager) getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
        if (lm != null) {
            String activeProvider = null;
            for (String p : new String[]{LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER, LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER}) {
                try {
                    if (lm.isProviderEnabled(p)) {
                        activeProvider = p;
                        break;
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
            if (activeProvider != null) {
                Toast.makeText(this, "📡 Acquiring live GPS fix for SOS...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                final CancellationSignal signal = new CancellationSignal();
                pendingSosCancellation = signal;
                Runnable timeout = () -> {
                    if (pendingSosCancellation != signal) return;
                    cancelPendingSosLocationRequest();
                    if (!isFinishing() && !isDestroyed()) {
                        dispatchSosShareIntent(cached);
                    }
                };
                pendingSosTimeout = timeout;
                mainHandler.postDelayed(timeout, SOS_GPS_TIMEOUT_MS);
                try {
                    LocationManagerCompat.getCurrentLocation(
                            lm,
                            activeProvider,
                            signal,
                            ContextCompat.getMainExecutor(this),
                            fresh -> {
                                if (pendingSosCancellation != signal) return;
                                cancelPendingSosLocationRequest();
                                if (!isFinishing() && !isDestroyed()) {
                                    dispatchSosShareIntent(fresh != null ? fresh : cached);
                                }
                            }
                    );
                    return;
                } catch (SecurityException | IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                    cancelPendingSosLocationRequest();
                }
            }
        }
        dispatchSosShareIntent(cached);
    }

    private void dispatchSosShareIntent(Location loc) {
        CampusRepository repo = CampusRepository.getInstance();
        String name = repo.getProfileField("student_name", "UM Student");
        String college = repo.getProfileField("college", "UM Campus");

        StringBuilder body = new StringBuilder("[UM-ClearFlow SOS] Emergency assistance needed!\n")
                .append("Sender: ").append(name).append(" (").append(college).append(")\n");
        if (loc != null) {
            long ageMin = Math.max(0, (System.currentTimeMillis() - loc.getTime()) / 60000);
            body.append(String.format(Locale.US, "Location: https://maps.google.com/?q=%.6f,%.6f\n",
                    loc.getLatitude(), loc.getLongitude()));
            body.append(String.format(Locale.US, "(accuracy ±%.0f m, fix taken %d min ago)",
                    loc.getAccuracy(), ageMin));
        } else {
            body.append("GPS location unavailable. Please call me.");
        }

        Intent send = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND);
        send.setType("text/plain");
        send.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, body.toString());
        startActivity(Intent.createChooser(send, "Share SOS Location via..."));
    }

    private void dial(String phone) {
        try {
            startActivity(new Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL, Uri.parse("tel:" + Uri.encode(phone))));
        } catch (android.content.ActivityNotFoundException e) {
            Toast.makeText(this, "No dialer app available. Emergency number: " + phone, Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
        }
    }

    private void cancelPendingSosLocationRequest() {
        CancellationSignal signal = pendingSosCancellation;
        Runnable timeout = pendingSosTimeout;
        pendingSosCancellation = null;
        pendingSosTimeout = null;
        if (timeout != null) mainHandler.removeCallbacks(timeout);
        if (signal != null) signal.cancel();
    }

    /**
     * targetSdk 36 => edge-to-edge is enforced on Android 15+. Without this the dark header and the SOS
     * button sit under the status bar / camera cut-out. The bottom bar is handled by
     * BottomNavigationView (app:paddingBottomSystemWindowInsets="true" in activity_main.xml).
     */
    private void applySystemBarInsets() {
        final View header = findViewById(R.id.topHeader);
        final int l = header.getPaddingLeft();
        final int t = header.getPaddingTop();
        final int r = header.getPaddingRight();
        final int b = header.getPaddingBottom();
        ViewCompat.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener(findViewById(R.id.main_root), (v, windowInsets) -> {
            Insets bars = windowInsets.getInsets(
                    WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars() | WindowInsetsCompat.Type.displayCutout());
            header.setPadding(l + bars.left, t + bars.top, r + bars.right, b);
            return windowInsets;
        });
        // Header is dark blue -> status-bar icons must be light
        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(getWindow(), getWindow().getDecorView())
                .setAppearanceLightStatusBars(false);
    }

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        CampusRepository.init(getApplicationContext());
        EdgeToEdge.enable(this);
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);

        setContentView(R.layout.activity_main);
        applySystemBarInsets();
        findViewById(R.id.btnTopSos).setOnClickListener(v -> showSosDialog());
        requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded();
        WeatherAlertScheduler.apply(getApplicationContext());

        FragmentManager fm = getSupportFragmentManager();
        if (savedInstanceState == null) {
            mapFragment = new MapFragment();
            reportFragment = new ReportFragment();
            weatherAiFragment = new WeatherAiFragment();
            profileFragment = new ProfileFragment();

            fm.beginTransaction()
                    .add(R.id.fragment_container, profileFragment, "PROFILE").hide(profileFragment)
                    .add(R.id.fragment_container, weatherAiFragment, "WEATHER").hide(weatherAiFragment)
                    .add(R.id.fragment_container, reportFragment, "REPORT").hide(reportFragment)
                    .add(R.id.fragment_container, mapFragment, "MAP")
                    .commit();
            activeFragment = mapFragment;
        } else {
            mapFragment = fm.findFragmentByTag("MAP");
            reportFragment = fm.findFragmentByTag("REPORT");
            weatherAiFragment = fm.findFragmentByTag("WEATHER");
            profileFragment = fm.findFragmentByTag("PROFILE");
            activeFragment = mapFragment;
            Fragment[] all = {mapFragment, reportFragment, weatherAiFragment, profileFragment};
            for (Fragment f : all) {
                if (f != null && !f.isHidden()) {
                    activeFragment = f;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        BottomNavigationView bottomNav = findViewById(R.id.bottom_navigation);
        bottomNav.setOnItemSelectedListener(item -> {
            int id = item.getItemId();
            Fragment target = mapFragment;
            if (id == R.id.nav_map) {
                target = mapFragment;
            } else if (id == R.id.nav_reports) {
                target = reportFragment;
            } else if (id == R.id.nav_weather_ai) {
                target = weatherAiFragment;
            } else if (id == R.id.nav_profile) {
                target = profileFragment;
            }

            if (target != null && target != activeFragment) {
                FragmentTransaction ft = getSupportFragmentManager().beginTransaction();
                ft.hide(activeFragment).show(target).commit();
                activeFragment = target;
            }
            return true;
        });
    }

    private void requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
                    != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                notifPermLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDestroy() {
        cancelPendingSosLocationRequest();
        mainHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
        super.onDestroy();
    }
}

                } else if (getContext() != null) {
                    Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Location permission denied. Choose a landmark as the route start.",
                            Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                }
            });

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater, @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        Context ctx = requireContext().getApplicationContext();
        SharedPreferences osmPrefs = ctx.getSharedPreferences(ctx.getPackageName() + "_preferences", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        IConfigurationProvider cfg = Configuration.getInstance();
        cfg.load(ctx, osmPrefs);
        cfg.setUserAgentValue(ctx.getPackageName());
        // App-private cache: no storage permission needed on modern Android
        File osmBase = new File(ctx.getCacheDir(), "osmdroid");
        cfg.setOsmdroidBasePath(osmBase);
        cfg.setOsmdroidTileCache(new File(osmBase, "tiles"));
        userAgent = ctx.getPackageName() + "/" + BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME;

        View view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_map, container, false);

        viewModel = new ViewModelProvider(requireActivity()).get(MapViewModel.class);

        tvMapSummary = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_map_summary);
        tvBroadcastBanner = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_broadcast_banner);
        tvRouteStatus = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_route_status);
        layoutRouteSelector = view.findViewById(R.id.layout_route_selector);
        spinnerOrigin = view.findViewById(R.id.spinner_origin);
        spinnerDestination = view.findViewById(R.id.spinner_destination);
        mapView = view.findViewById(R.id.osm_map);

        setupMap();
        setupSpinners();
        setupControls(view);
        observeViewModel();

        return view;
    }

    private void setupMap() {
        mapView.setTileSource(TileSourceFactory.MAPNIK);
        mapView.setMultiTouchControls(true);
        mapView.getController().setZoom(15.6);
        mapView.getController().setCenter(new GeoPoint(3.1245, 101.6555));
    }

    private void setupSpinners() {
        spinnerOrigin.setAdapter(new ArrayAdapter<>(
                requireContext(), android.R.layout.simple_spinner_dropdown_item, originNames));

        List<CampusRepository.ShelterInfo> shelters = CampusRepository.getInstance().getCampusShelters();
        List<String> destNames = new ArrayList<>();
        for (CampusRepository.ShelterInfo s : shelters) {
            destNames.add(s.name);
        }
        spinnerDestination.setAdapter(new ArrayAdapter<>(
                requireContext(), android.R.layout.simple_spinner_dropdown_item, destNames));

        Integer savedO = viewModel.getOriginIndex().getValue();
        Integer savedD = viewModel.getDestinationIndex().getValue();
        if (savedO != null && savedO >= 0 && savedO < originNames.length) spinnerOrigin.setSelection(savedO, false);
        if (savedD != null && savedD >= 0 && savedD < destNames.size()) spinnerDestination.setSelection(savedD, false);

        AdapterView.OnItemSelectedListener routeChangeListener = new AdapterView.OnItemSelectedListener() {
            @Override
            public void onItemSelected(AdapterView<?> parent, View v, int position, long id) {
                viewModel.setOriginIndex(spinnerOrigin.getSelectedItemPosition());
                viewModel.setDestinationIndex(spinnerDestination.getSelectedItemPosition());
                renderMapOverlays();
            }

            @Override
            public void onNothingSelected(AdapterView<?> parent) {}
        };
        spinnerOrigin.setOnItemSelectedListener(routeChangeListener);
        spinnerDestination.setOnItemSelectedListener(routeChangeListener);
    }

    private void setupControls(View view) {
        btnAll = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_filter_all);
        btnCritical = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_filter_critical);
        Button btnMyLocation = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_my_location);
        CheckBox cbShelters = view.findViewById(R.id.cb_show_shelters);
        CheckBox cbRoute = view.findViewById(R.id.cb_evac_route);
        cbShelters.setChecked(Boolean.TRUE.equals(viewModel.getSheltersVisible().getValue()));
        cbRoute.setChecked(Boolean.TRUE.equals(viewModel.getRouteEnabled().getValue()));

        btnAll.setOnClickListener(v -> viewModel.setFilter("All"));
        btnCritical.setOnClickListener(v -> viewModel.setFilter("Critical"));
        btnMyLocation.setOnClickListener(v -> locateUser());

        cbShelters.setOnCheckedChangeListener((buttonView, isChecked) ->
                viewModel.setSheltersVisible(isChecked));

        cbRoute.setOnCheckedChangeListener((buttonView, isChecked) -> {
            layoutRouteSelector.setVisibility(isChecked ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
            viewModel.setRouteEnabled(isChecked);
        });
    }

    private void observeViewModel() {
        viewModel.getReports().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), reports -> renderMapOverlays());
        viewModel.getSelectedFilter().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), filter -> {
            updateFilterButtonsHighlight(filter);
            renderMapOverlays();
        });
        viewModel.getSheltersVisible().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), visible -> renderMapOverlays());
        viewModel.getRouteEnabled().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), enabled -> {
            layoutRouteSelector.setVisibility(Boolean.TRUE.equals(enabled) ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
            renderMapOverlays();
        });
        viewModel.getBroadcastBanner().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), msg -> {
            if (msg != null && !msg.trim().isEmpty()) {
                tvBroadcastBanner.setText("📢 ADMIN ALERT: " + msg);
                tvBroadcastBanner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            } else {
                tvBroadcastBanner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            }
        });
    }

    private void updateFilterButtonsHighlight(String filter) {
        boolean criticalOnly = "Critical".equalsIgnoreCase(filter);
        if (btnAll != null && btnCritical != null) {
            btnAll.setAlpha(criticalOnly ? 0.55f : 1.0f);
            btnCritical.setAlpha(criticalOnly ? 1.0f : 0.55f);
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ GPS (FR 4.3)

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private void locateUser() {
        Context ctx = getContext();
        if (ctx == null) return;
        boolean granted = ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(ctx, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)
                == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
                || ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(ctx, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION)
                == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED;
        if (!granted) {
            locPermLauncher.launch(new String[]{
                    Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                    Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION});
            return;
        }
        LocationManager lm = (LocationManager) ctx.getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
        if (lm == null) return;

        Location cached = null;
        for (String p : new String[]{LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER,
                LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER, LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER}) {
            try {
                Location l = lm.getLastKnownLocation(p);
                if (l != null && (cached == null || l.getTime() > cached.getTime())) cached = l;
            } catch (SecurityException | IllegalArgumentException ignored) {}
        }
        if (cached != null && System.currentTimeMillis() - cached.getTime() <= MAX_FIX_AGE_MS) {
            applyUserLocation(cached);
            return;
        }

        String provider = null;
        for (String p : new String[]{LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER, LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER}) {
            try {
                if (lm.isProviderEnabled(p)) {
                    provider = p;
                    break;
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }
        final Location fallback = cached;
        if (provider == null) {
            if (fallback != null) applyUserLocation(fallback);
            else Toast.makeText(ctx, "Turn on device location (GPS) and try again.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
            return;
        }

        cancelLocate();
        final CancellationSignal signal = new CancellationSignal();
        locateSignal = signal;
        Toast.makeText(ctx, "📡 Acquiring GPS fix...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        Runnable timeout = () -> {
            if (locateSignal != signal) return;
            cancelLocate();
            if (!isAdded()) return;
            if (fallback != null) applyUserLocation(fallback);
            else Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "No GPS fix yet. Try again outdoors.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
        };
        locateTimeout = timeout;
        mainHandler.postDelayed(timeout, LOCATE_TIMEOUT_MS);
        try {
            LocationManagerCompat.getCurrentLocation(lm, provider, signal,
                    ContextCompat.getMainExecutor(ctx), fresh -> {
                        if (locateSignal != signal) return;
                        cancelLocate();
                        if (!isAdded()) return;
                        Location use = fresh != null ? fresh : fallback;
                        if (use != null) applyUserLocation(use);
                        else Toast.makeText(requireContext(), "No GPS fix yet. Try again outdoors.",
                                Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                    });
        } catch (SecurityException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
            cancelLocate();
            if (fallback != null) applyUserLocation(fallback);
        }
    }

    private void cancelLocate() {
        CancellationSignal s = locateSignal;
        Runnable t = locateTimeout;
        locateSignal = null;
        locateTimeout = null;
        if (t != null) mainHandler.removeCallbacks(t);
        if (s != null) s.cancel();
    }

    private void applyUserLocation(@NonNull Location loc) {
        myLocation = new GeoPoint(loc.getLatitude(), loc.getLongitude());
        myAccuracyM = loc.getAccuracy();
        if (mapView == null || !isAdded()) return;
        mapView.getController().animateTo(myLocation);
        spinnerOrigin.setSelection(MY_LOCATION_INDEX); // listener re-renders
        renderMapOverlays();
        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), String.format(Locale.US,
                "📍 Location set (±%.0f m)", myAccuracyM), Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Rendering

    private static double radiusOf(HazardReport h) {
        return "Critical".equalsIgnoreCase(h.severity) ? 95.0 : 55.0;
    }

    private void renderMapOverlays() {
        if (mapView == null) return;
        mapView.getOverlays().clear();

        List<HazardReport> allReports = viewModel.getReports().getValue();
        if (allReports == null) allReports = new ArrayList<>();

        String filter = viewModel.getSelectedFilter().getValue();
        boolean showShelters = Boolean.TRUE.equals(viewModel.getSheltersVisible().getValue());
        boolean showRoute = Boolean.TRUE.equals(viewModel.getRouteEnabled().getValue());

        int activeCount = 0;
        int criticalCount = 0;

        // 1. Graded hazard halos + markers (FR 4.1)
        for (HazardReport r : allReports) {
            if ("Resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(r.status)) continue;
            if ("Critical".equalsIgnoreCase(filter) && !"Critical".equalsIgnoreCase(r.severity)) {
                continue;
            }
            activeCount++;
            boolean isCritical = "Critical".equalsIgnoreCase(r.severity);
            if (isCritical) criticalCount++;

            GeoPoint pt = new GeoPoint(r.latitude, r.longitude);

            Polygon outerHalo = new Polygon();
            outerHalo.setPoints(Polygon.pointsAsCircle(pt, isCritical ? 145.0 : 85.0));
            outerHalo.getFillPaint().setColor(isCritical
                    ? Color.argb(45, 220, 38, 38)
                    : Color.argb(35, 245, 158, 11));
            outerHalo.getOutlinePaint().setColor(Color.TRANSPARENT);
            mapView.getOverlays().add(outerHalo);

            Polygon coreCircle = new Polygon();
            coreCircle.setPoints(Polygon.pointsAsCircle(pt, radiusOf(r)));
            coreCircle.getFillPaint().setColor(isCritical
                    ? Color.argb(85, 220, 38, 38)
                    : Color.argb(75, 245, 158, 11));
            coreCircle.getOutlinePaint().setColor(isCritical
                    ? Color.rgb(185, 28, 28)
                    : Color.rgb(217, 119, 6));
            coreCircle.getOutlinePaint().setStrokeWidth(3f);
            mapView.getOverlays().add(coreCircle);

            Marker marker = new Marker(mapView);
            marker.setPosition(pt);
            marker.setAnchor(Marker.ANCHOR_CENTER, Marker.ANCHOR_BOTTOM);
            marker.setTitle("[" + r.severity.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT) + "] " + r.title);
            marker.setSnippet("Type: " + r.hazardType + " | Status: " + r.status
                    + " | 👍 " + r.upvotes + " | ✅ Clear Votes: " + r.resolveVotes + "/3\n"
                    + r.aiTriageNote);
            mapView.getOverlays().add(marker);
        }

        // 2. Shelters (FR 4.1)
        if (showShelters) {
            for (CampusRepository.ShelterInfo shelter : CampusRepository.getInstance().getCampusShelters()) {
                Marker sm = new Marker(mapView);
                sm.setPosition(new GeoPoint(shelter.lat, shelter.lon));
                sm.setAnchor(Marker.ANCHOR_CENTER, Marker.ANCHOR_CENTER);
                sm.setTitle("🏛️ [EVACUATION SHELTER] " + shelter.name);
                sm.setSnippet(shelter.description);
                sm.setIcon(ContextCompat.getDrawable(requireContext(), android.R.drawable.ic_dialog_info));
                mapView.getOverlays().add(sm);
            }
        }

        // 3. Safe route (FR 4.2)
        if (showRoute) {
            drawDynamicSafeRoute(allReports);
        }

        // 4. User position (FR 4.3)
        if (myLocation != null) {
            Marker me = new Marker(mapView);
            me.setPosition(myLocation);
            me.setAnchor(Marker.ANCHOR_CENTER, Marker.ANCHOR_CENTER);
            me.setTitle("📍 You are here");
            me.setSnippet(String.format(Locale.US, "Accuracy ±%.0f m", myAccuracyM));
            me.setIcon(ContextCompat.getDrawable(requireContext(), android.R.drawable.ic_menu_mylocation));
            mapView.getOverlays().add(me);
        }

        tvMapSummary.setText(String.format(Locale.US,
                "Visible Active Reports: %d (%d Critical) • Shelters: %s",
                activeCount, criticalCount, showShelters ? "ON" : "OFF"));

        mapView.invalidate();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Routing (FR 4.2)

    private static String routingService(String mobilityPref) {
        if (mobilityPref.contains("Cycling")) return "routed-bike";
        if (mobilityPref.contains("Driving") || mobilityPref.contains("Bus")) return "routed-car";
        return "routed-foot"; // walking / wheelchair
    }

    private static String hazardSignature(List<HazardReport> reports) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (HazardReport h : reports) {
            if ("Resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(h.status)) continue;
            sb.append(h.id).append(h.severity)
                    .append(String.format(Locale.US, "%.5f,%.5f;", h.latitude, h.longitude));
        }
        return String.valueOf(sb.toString().hashCode());
    }

    private void drawDynamicSafeRoute(List<HazardReport> allReports) {
        int oIdx = spinnerOrigin.getSelectedItemPosition();
        int dIdx = spinnerDestination.getSelectedItemPosition();
        if (oIdx < 0 || oIdx >= originNames.length) oIdx = 0;

        List<CampusRepository.ShelterInfo> shelters = CampusRepository.getInstance().getCampusShelters();
        if (dIdx < 0 || dIdx >= shelters.size()) dIdx = 0;

        final GeoPoint start;
        String originKey;
        if (oIdx == MY_LOCATION_INDEX) {
            if (myLocation == null) {
                tvRouteStatus.setText("Tap “📍 My Location” to use your GPS position, or pick a landmark as the starting point.");
                return;
            }
            start = myLocation;
            originKey = String.format(Locale.US, "gps%.4f,%.4f", myLocation.getLatitude(), myLocation.getLongitude());
        } else {
            start = originPoints[oIdx];
            originKey = "lm" + oIdx;
        }
        final CampusRepository.ShelterInfo target = shelters.get(dIdx);
        final GeoPoint end = new GeoPoint(target.lat, target.lon);
        final String mobility = CampusRepository.getInstance()
                .getProfileField("mobility_pref", "Walking (Pedestrian / Covered Walkway)");
        final String service = routingService(mobility);
        final String key = originKey + "|" + dIdx + "|" + service + "|" + hazardSignature(allReports);

        if (cachedRoute != null && key.equals(cachedRouteKey)) {
            paintRoute(cachedRoute, target.name, mobility);
            return;
        }
        if (key.equals(pendingRouteKey)) {
            tvRouteStatus.setText("⏳ Calculating a road route that avoids reported hazards...");
            return;
        }

        pendingRouteKey = key;
        final long requestId = ++routeRequestId;
        tvRouteStatus.setText("⏳ Calculating a road route that avoids reported hazards...");
        final List<HazardReport> snapshot = new ArrayList<>(allReports); // snapshot objects are deep copies

        routeExecutor.execute(() -> {
            RouteResult result;
            try {
                result = computeRoute(start, end, service, snapshot);
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                result = new RouteResult(Arrays.asList(start, end), ROUTE_STRAIGHT_ESTIMATE, "", -1, -1);
            }
            final RouteResult finalResult = result;
            mainHandler.post(() -> {
                if (!isAdded() || mapView == null || requestId != routeRequestId) return;
                cachedRoute = finalResult;
                cachedRouteKey = key;
                pendingRouteKey = null;
                renderMapOverlays();
            });
        });
    }

    /** Runs on a background thread. */
    private RouteResult computeRoute(GeoPoint start, GeoPoint end, String service, List<HazardReport> reports) {
        // Hazards sitting on the origin/destination themselves cannot be bypassed: warn, don't block.
        List<HazardReport> avoidable = new ArrayList<>();
        StringBuilder terminalNote = new StringBuilder();
        for (HazardReport h : reports) {
            if ("Resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(h.status)) continue;
            GeoPoint hp = new GeoPoint(h.latitude, h.longitude);
            double r = radiusOf(h);
            if (hp.distanceToAsDouble(start) < r || hp.distanceToAsDouble(end) < r) {
                if (terminalNote.length() > 0) terminalNote.append("; ");
                terminalNote.append(h.title);
            } else {
                avoidable.add(h);
            }
        }
        String terminal = terminalNote.length() > 0 ? terminalNote.toString() : "";

        List<RoadRoute> roads = fetchRoadRoutes(service, Arrays.asList(start, end), true);
        HazardReport firstHit = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < roads.size(); i++) {
            HazardReport hit = findHit(roads.get(i).points, avoidable);
            if (hit == null) {
                return new RouteResult(roads.get(i).points,
                        i == 0 ? ROUTE_SAFE_ROAD : ROUTE_DETOUR_ROAD,
                        i == 0 ? terminal : (firstHit != null ? firstHit.title + " (" + firstHit.hazardType + ")" : terminal),
                        roads.get(i).distanceM, roads.get(i).durationS);
            }
            if (i == 0) firstHit = hit;
        }

        if (!roads.isEmpty() && firstHit != null) {
            // Direct road(s) blocked: re-route through a via-point offset sideways from the hazard
            for (GeoPoint via : detourWaypoints(firstHit, start, end)) {
                List<RoadRoute> detour = fetchRoadRoutes(service, Arrays.asList(start, via, end), false);
                if (!detour.isEmpty() && findHit(detour.get(0).points, avoidable) == null) {
                    return new RouteResult(detour.get(0).points, ROUTE_DETOUR_ROAD,
                            firstHit.title + " (" + firstHit.hazardType + ")",
                            detour.get(0).distanceM, detour.get(0).durationS);
                }
            }
            return new RouteResult(roads.get(0).points, ROUTE_BLOCKED,
                    firstHit.title + " (" + firstHit.hazardType + ")",
                    roads.get(0).distanceM, roads.get(0).durationS);
        }

        // Road routing service unreachable (offline): geometric corridor estimate, clearly labelled
        List<List<GeoPoint>> candidates = geometricCandidates(start, end);
        HazardReport directHit = null;
        for (int i = 0; i < candidates.size(); i++) {
            HazardReport hit = findHit(candidates.get(i), avoidable);
            if (hit == null) {
                return new RouteResult(candidates.get(i), ROUTE_STRAIGHT_ESTIMATE, terminal, -1, -1);
            }
            if (i == 0) directHit = hit;
        }
        return new RouteResult(candidates.get(0), ROUTE_BLOCKED,
                directHit != null ? directHit.title + " (" + directHit.hazardType + ")" : "", -1, -1);
    }

    private List<List<GeoPoint>> geometricCandidates(GeoPoint start, GeoPoint end) {
        double midLat = (start.getLatitude() + end.getLatitude()) / 2.0;
        double midLon = (start.getLongitude() + end.getLongitude()) / 2.0;
        double dLat = end.getLatitude() - start.getLatitude();
        double dLon = end.getLongitude() - start.getLongitude();
        double len = Math.hypot(dLat, dLon);
        double normLat = len > 1e-5 ? -dLon / len : 0.0020;
        double normLon = len > 1e-5 ? dLat / len : 0.0020;

        List<List<GeoPoint>> c = new ArrayList<>();
        c.add(Arrays.asList(start, new GeoPoint(midLat, midLon), end));
        c.add(Arrays.asList(start,
                new GeoPoint(midLat + normLat * 0.0020, midLon + normLon * 0.0020), end));
        c.add(Arrays.asList(start,
                new GeoPoint(midLat - normLat * 0.0022, midLon - normLon * 0.0022), end));
        return c;
    }

    private List<GeoPoint> detourWaypoints(HazardReport h, GeoPoint start, GeoPoint end) {
        double lat = h.latitude;
        double lon = h.longitude;
        double cosLat = Math.cos(Math.toRadians(lat));
        double dLat = end.getLatitude() - start.getLatitude();
        double dLonScaled = (end.getLongitude() - start.getLongitude()) * cosLat;
        double len = Math.hypot(dLat, dLonScaled);
        if (len < 1e-9) return Collections.emptyList();
        double nLat = -dLonScaled / len;
        double nLon = dLat / len;
        double offMeters = radiusOf(h) + 80.0;
        double offLat = offMeters / 111320.0;
        double offLon = offMeters / (111320.0 * cosLat);
        return Arrays.asList(
                new GeoPoint(lat + nLat * offLat, lon + nLon * offLon),
                new GeoPoint(lat - nLat * offLat, lon - nLon * offLon));
    }

    /** Background thread only. Returns an empty list on any failure. */
    private List<RoadRoute> fetchRoadRoutes(String service, List<GeoPoint> coords, boolean alternatives) {
        List<RoadRoute> out = new ArrayList<>();
        HttpURLConnection conn = null;
        try {
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(ROUTING_BASE).append(service).append("/route/v1/driving/");
            for (int i = 0; i < coords.size(); i++) {
                if (i > 0) sb.append(';');
                sb.append(String.format(Locale.US, "%.6f,%.6f",
                        coords.get(i).getLongitude(), coords.get(i).getLatitude()));
            }
            sb.append("?overview=full&geometries=geojson");
            if (alternatives && coords.size() == 2) sb.append("&alternatives=true");

            conn = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(sb.toString()).openConnection();
            conn.setRequestMethod("GET");
            conn.setConnectTimeout(4000);
            conn.setReadTimeout(8000);
            conn.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", userAgent);
            if (conn.getResponseCode() != HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) return out;

            StringBuilder body = new StringBuilder();
            try (BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(
                    new InputStreamReader(conn.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
                String line;
                while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) body.append(line);
            }
            JSONObject root = new JSONObject(body.toString());
            if (!"Ok".equals(root.optString("code"))) return out;
            JSONArray routes = root.getJSONArray("routes");
            for (int i = 0; i < routes.length(); i++) {
                JSONObject r = routes.getJSONObject(i);
                JSONArray arr = r.getJSONObject("geometry").getJSONArray("coordinates");
                List<GeoPoint> pts = new ArrayList<>(arr.length());
                for (int k = 0; k < arr.length(); k++) {
                    JSONArray c = arr.getJSONArray(k);
                    pts.add(new GeoPoint(c.getDouble(1), c.getDouble(0)));
                }
                if (pts.size() >= 2) {
                    out.add(new RoadRoute(pts, r.optDouble("distance", -1), r.optDouble("duration", -1)));
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            out.clear();
        } finally {
            if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
        }
        return out;
    }

    private void paintRoute(RouteResult r, String targetName, String mobility) {
        Polyline line = new Polyline();
        line.setPoints(r.points);
        int color;
        switch (r.state) {
            case ROUTE_SAFE_ROAD: color = Color.rgb(22, 163, 74); break;
            case ROUTE_DETOUR_ROAD: color = Color.rgb(37, 99, 235); break;
            case ROUTE_STRAIGHT_ESTIMATE: color = Color.rgb(217, 119, 6); break;
            default: color = Color.rgb(220, 38, 38); break;
        }
        line.getOutlinePaint().setColor(color);
        line.getOutlinePaint().setStrokeWidth(r.state == ROUTE_BLOCKED ? 8f : 11f);
        mapView.getOverlays().add(line);

        String stats = r.distanceM >= 0
                ? String.format(Locale.US, "%.0f m, ~%.0f min", r.distanceM, Math.max(1.0, r.durationS / 60.0))
                : "distance unknown";
        String access = mobility.contains("Wheelchair")
                ? " Ramps/steps/accessibility are NOT checked by this route." : "";
        String terminal = (r.state == ROUTE_SAFE_ROAD && !r.note.isEmpty())
                ? " ⚠️ Hazard reported at start/destination: " + r.note + "." : "";
        String verify = " Based only on reports on this device; verify conditions and official UM guidance before moving.";

        switch (r.state) {
            case ROUTE_SAFE_ROAD:
                tvRouteStatus.setText("🟢 Road route avoiding recorded hazards (" + stats + ") → " + targetName
                        + "." + terminal + access + verify);
                break;
            case ROUTE_DETOUR_ROAD:
                tvRouteStatus.setText("🔵 The direct road crosses [" + r.note + "]. Detour road route shown ("
                        + stats + ") → " + targetName + "." + access + verify);
                break;
            case ROUTE_STRAIGHT_ESTIMATE:
                tvRouteStatus.setText("🟠 Road routing unavailable (offline?). Straight-line corridor estimate only → "
                        + targetName + "; it may cross buildings or water." + access + verify);
                break;
            default:
                tvRouteStatus.setText("🚨 Every candidate route crosses a locally recorded active hazard"
                        + (r.note.isEmpty() ? "" : " [" + r.note + "]")
                        + ". Follow current official UM instructions; if in immediate danger use SOS.");
                break;
        }
    }

    /** Densify to 10 m so a long segment cannot jump over a hazard circle. */
    private List<GeoPoint> densifyRoute(List<GeoPoint> waypoints, double stepMeters) {
        List<GeoPoint> dense = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < waypoints.size() - 1; i++) {
            GeoPoint a = waypoints.get(i);
            GeoPoint b = waypoints.get(i + 1);
            double dist = a.distanceToAsDouble(b);
            int steps = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(dist / stepMeters));
            for (int s = 0; s < steps; s++) {
                double ratio = (double) s / steps;
                dense.add(new GeoPoint(
                        a.getLatitude() + ratio * (b.getLatitude() - a.getLatitude()),
                        a.getLongitude() + ratio * (b.getLongitude() - a.getLongitude())));
            }
        }
        if (!waypoints.isEmpty()) {
            dense.add(waypoints.get(waypoints.size() - 1));
        }
        return dense;
    }

    private HazardReport findHit(List<GeoPoint> route, List<HazardReport> hazards) {
        List<GeoPoint> dense = densifyRoute(route, 10.0);
        for (HazardReport h : hazards) {
            GeoPoint hp = new GeoPoint(h.latitude, h.longitude);
            double radius = radiusOf(h);
            for (GeoPoint p : dense) {
                if (p.distanceToAsDouble(hp) < radius) return h;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Lifecycle

    @Override
    public void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        if (mapView != null) mapView.onResume();
    }

    @Override
    public void onPause() {
        super.onPause();
        if (mapView != null) mapView.onPause();
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        cancelLocate();
        routeRequestId++;           // drop any in-flight route result
        pendingRouteKey = null;
        mainHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
        if (mapView != null) {
            mapView.getOverlays().clear();
            mapView.onDetach();
            mapView = null;
        }
        super.onDestroyView();
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroy() {
        routeExecutor.shutdownNow();
        super.onDestroy();
    }
}

        setSpinnerToValue(spinnerCollege, colleges, repo.getProfileField("college", colleges[0]));
        setSpinnerToValue(spinnerMobility, mobilityOptions, repo.getProfileField("mobility_pref", mobilityOptions[0]));
    }

    private void setSpinnerToValue(Spinner spinner, String[] items, String target) {
        for (int i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
            if (items[i].equalsIgnoreCase(target)) {
                spinner.setSelection(i);
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private void setupActions(View view) {
        Button btnSaveProfile = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_save_profile);
        Button btnPublishBroadcast = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_publish_broadcast);
        Button btnClearBroadcast = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_clear_broadcast);
        btnExportCsv = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_export_csv);

        // FR 1.1 & 1.2: 校验 @siswa.um.edu.my 域名并持久化个人档案
        btnSaveProfile.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            String email = etEmail.getText().toString().trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            String name = etName.getText().toString().trim();
            String matric = etMatric.getText().toString().trim();
            String emName = etEmergencyName.getText().toString().trim();
            String emPhone = etEmergencyPhone.getText().toString().trim();

            if (!Patterns.EMAIL_ADDRESS.matcher(email).matches()
                    || !email.endsWith("@siswa.um.edu.my")) {
                tvAuthStatus.setText("❌ Domain Error: Must use an official @siswa.um.edu.my email!");
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Invalid domain! Please use @siswa.um.edu.my", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                return;
            }
            if (name.isEmpty() || matric.isEmpty()) {
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Please fill in your Name and Matric ID.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                return;
            }

            CampusRepository.getInstance().saveUserProfile(
                    email, name, matric,
                    spinnerFaculty.getSelectedItem().toString(),
                    spinnerCollege.getSelectedItem().toString(),
                    emName, emPhone,
                    spinnerMobility.getSelectedItem().toString()
            );

            tvAuthStatus.setText("✅ Profile saved; email domain checked locally (not independently verified): "
                    + email + " (" + matric + ")");
            Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Profile, Emergency Contact & Mobility Preferences saved!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        });

        // FR 1.3: 管理员角色切换必须验证 PIN 码 (默认 UM2026)
        btnToggleAdmin.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            if (CampusRepository.getInstance().isAdmin()) {
                viewModel.switchToStudent();
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Switched back to Student / Staff role.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            } else {
                showAdminPinDialog();
            }
        });

        btnPublishBroadcast.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            String msg = etBroadcastMsg.getText().toString().trim();
            if (msg.isEmpty()) {
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Please enter a broadcast message.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                return;
            }
            viewModel.sendBroadcast(msg);
            Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Local demo notice saved and shown on this device only.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        });

        btnClearBroadcast.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            etBroadcastMsg.setText("");
            viewModel.sendBroadcast("");
            Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Broadcast banner cleared.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        });

        // FR 5.2: 导出并分享真实 CSV 报告
        btnExportCsv.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            if (!CampusRepository.getInstance().isAdmin()) {
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Admin role required.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                return;
            }
            String csvData = viewModel.exportCsvContent();
            Intent sendIntent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND);
            sendIntent.setType("text/csv");
            sendIntent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, "UM-ClearFlow Hazard Report Export (CSV)");
            sendIntent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, csvData);
            try {
                java.io.File exportDir = new java.io.File(requireContext().getCacheDir(), "exports");
                if (!exportDir.exists()) exportDir.mkdirs();
                java.io.File csvFile = new java.io.File(exportDir, "um_clearflow_reports.csv");
                try (java.io.FileOutputStream fos = new java.io.FileOutputStream(csvFile)) {
                    fos.write(csvData.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                }
                android.net.Uri uri = androidx.core.content.FileProvider.getUriForFile(
                        requireContext(), requireContext().getPackageName() + ".fileprovider", csvFile);
                sendIntent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri);
                sendIntent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION);
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            startActivity(Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, "Export CSV Report via..."));
        });
    }

    private void showAdminPinDialog() {
        EditText inputPin = new EditText(requireContext());
        inputPin.setHint("Enter JPPHB staff authorization PIN");
        inputPin.setInputType(InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT | InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD);

        new AlertDialog.Builder(requireContext())
                .setTitle("🔐 JPPHB Admin Verification")
                .setMessage("Enter staff authorization code to unlock ticket status updates and campus broadcast:")
                .setView(inputPin)
                .setPositiveButton("Verify", (dialog, which) -> {
                    String pin = inputPin.getText().toString();
                    if (viewModel.verifyAdminPin(pin)) {
                        Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Demo admin view unlocked on this device only.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    } else if (CampusRepository.getInstance().getPinLockoutRemainingMs() > 0) {
                        long sec = (CampusRepository.getInstance().getPinLockoutRemainingMs() + 999) / 1000;
                        Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Too many wrong PINs. Try again in " + sec + "s.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(getContext(), "❌ Invalid Admin PIN! Access Denied.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                    }
                })
                .setNegativeButton("Cancel", null)
                .show();
    }

    private void observeViewModel() {
        viewModel.getUserRole().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), role -> {
            boolean isAdmin = "JPPHB Maintenance Admin".equals(role);
            tvRoleStatus.setText("Current Role: " + role);
            btnToggleAdmin.setText(isAdmin
                    ? "🔓 Logout Admin (Return to Student View)"
                    : "🔐 Authenticate JPPHB Admin");
            layoutAdminBroadcast.setVisibility(isAdmin ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
            if (btnExportCsv != null) {
                btnExportCsv.setVisibility(isAdmin ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
            }
        });

        viewModel.getReports().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), reports -> {
            tvHotspotRanking.setText(viewModel.getHotspotStats());
        });
    }

    private void setupBackgroundAlerts(View view) {
        swBgAlerts = view.findViewById(R.id.sw_bg_alerts);
        tvBgAlertStatus = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_bg_alert_status);
        Button btnRunNow = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_bg_check_now);
        Button btnSimulate = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_bg_simulate);
        final Context app = requireContext().getApplicationContext();
        final AlertPrefs prefs = new AlertPrefs(app);

        swBgAlerts.setChecked(prefs.isEnabled());
        swBgAlerts.setOnCheckedChangeListener((b, on) -> {
            prefs.setEnabled(on);
            WeatherAlertScheduler.apply(app);
            refreshBackgroundAlertStatus();
        });
        btnRunNow.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            WeatherAlertScheduler.runNow(app, false);
            Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Background check queued; the result appears below in a few seconds.",
                    Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            tvBgAlertStatus.postDelayed(this::refreshBackgroundAlertStatus, 4000);
        });
        if (BuildConfig.DEBUG) {
            btnSimulate.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                WeatherAlertScheduler.runNow(app, true);
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Simulated squall queued. Press Home: the notification comes from the background worker.",
                        Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                tvBgAlertStatus.postDelayed(this::refreshBackgroundAlertStatus, 4000);
            });
        } else {
            btnSimulate.setVisibility(View.GONE);
        }
        refreshBackgroundAlertStatus();
    }

    private void refreshBackgroundAlertStatus() {
        if (tvBgAlertStatus == null || !isAdded()) return;
        AlertPrefs prefs = new AlertPrefs(requireContext());
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        if (!androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat.from(requireContext()).areNotificationsEnabled()) {
            sb.append("⚠️ Notifications are blocked for this app, so alerts cannot be shown. Enable them in system settings.\n");
        }
        if (!prefs.isEnabled()) {
            sb.append("Background checks are off.");
        } else if (prefs.lastCheckMs() == 0L) {
            sb.append("No background check has run yet (the first one runs within about 15 minutes).");
        } else {
            sb.append("Last check ")
                    .append(new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss", Locale.getDefault()).format(new Date(prefs.lastCheckMs())))
                    .append(" — ").append(prefs.lastSummary());
        }
        tvBgAlertStatus.setText(sb.toString());
    }

    @Override
    public void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        refreshBackgroundAlertStatus();
    }

    @Override
    public void onHiddenChanged(boolean hidden) {   // tabs are shown/hidden, not recreated
        super.onHiddenChanged(hidden);
        if (!hidden) refreshBackgroundAlertStatus();
    }
}

package com.um.clearflow;

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\ReportAdapter.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import android.content.Context;
import android.graphics.Bitmap;
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.graphics.Typeface;
import android.util.LruCache;
import android.util.TypedValue;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Button;
import android.widget.ImageView;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.TextView;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.DiffUtil;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.ListAdapter;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.lang.ref.WeakReference;

/**
 * Hazard report list adapter. DiffUtil only rebinds cards whose content actually changed,
 * instead of tearing down and re-inflating every card on each LiveData emission.
 */
public class ReportAdapter extends ListAdapter<HazardReport, ReportAdapter.CardHolder> {

    private static final ExecutorService PHOTO_EXECUTOR = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
    private static final LruCache<String, Bitmap> PHOTO_CACHE = new LruCache<String, Bitmap>(
            (int) (Runtime.getRuntime().maxMemory() / 8L)) {
        @Override
        protected int sizeOf(@NonNull String key, @NonNull Bitmap value) {
            return Math.max(1, value.getByteCount());
        }
    };

    public interface Actions {
        void onUpvote(HazardReport r);
        void onVoteResolve(HazardReport r);
        void onAdminStatus(HazardReport r);
    }

    private final Actions actions;
    private boolean isAdmin;

    public ReportAdapter(Actions actions) {
        super(DIFF);
        this.actions = actions;
    }

    /** Role affects every card's admin button, so rebind all when it changes. */
    @SuppressWarnings("NotifyDataSetChanged")
    public void setAdmin(boolean admin) {
        if (admin != isAdmin) {
            isAdmin = admin;
            notifyDataSetChanged();
        }
    }

    private static final DiffUtil.ItemCallback<HazardReport> DIFF = new DiffUtil.ItemCallback<HazardReport>() {
        @Override
        public boolean areItemsTheSame(@NonNull HazardReport a, @NonNull HazardReport b) {
            return Objects.equals(a.id, b.id);
        }

        @Override
        public boolean areContentsTheSame(@NonNull HazardReport a, @NonNull HazardReport b) {
            return Objects.equals(a.title, b.title)
                    && Objects.equals(a.description, b.description)
                    && Objects.equals(a.locationName, b.locationName)
                    && Objects.equals(a.severity, b.severity)
                    && Objects.equals(a.status, b.status)
                    && Objects.equals(a.timestamp, b.timestamp)
                    && Objects.equals(a.reporterEmail, b.reporterEmail)
                    && Objects.equals(a.aiTriageNote, b.aiTriageNote)
                    && Objects.equals(a.timeline, b.timeline)
                    && Objects.equals(a.photoUri, b.photoUri)
                    && a.upvotes == b.upvotes
                    && a.resolveVotes == b.resolveVotes
                    && a.latitude == b.latitude
                    && a.longitude == b.longitude;
        }
    };

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public CardHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
        return new CardHolder(parent.getContext());
    }

    @Override
    public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull CardHolder h, int position) {
        HazardReport r = getItem(position);
        CampusRepository repo = CampusRepository.getInstance();
        boolean resolved = "Resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(r.status);

        h.header.setText(String.format(Locale.US, "[%s] %s • %s", r.severity, r.title, r.status));
        h.header.setTextColor("Critical".equalsIgnoreCase(r.severity)
                ? Color.rgb(185, 28, 28) : Color.rgb(15, 23, 42));
        h.meta.setText(String.format(Locale.US, "📍 %s (%.4f, %.4f)\n🕒 %s | Reporter: %s",
                r.locationName, r.latitude, r.longitude, r.timestamp, r.reporterEmail));
        h.body.setText(r.description + "\nRule-based estimate: " + r.aiTriageNote);

        bindPhoto(h.photo, r.photoUri);

        h.timeline.setText("📋 Timeline:\n" + r.timeline);

        boolean upvoted = repo.hasUpvoted(r.id);
        h.upvote.setText((upvoted ? "👍 Upvoted (" : "👍 Upvote (") + r.upvotes + ")");
        h.upvote.setEnabled(!upvoted);
        h.upvote.setOnClickListener(v -> actions.onUpvote(r));

        boolean votedResolve = repo.hasVotedResolve(r.id);
        if (resolved) {
            h.resolve.setText("✅ Resolved");
            h.resolve.setEnabled(false);
        } else if (votedResolve && BuildConfig.DEBUG) {
            h.resolve.setText("🌤️ +Demo Vote (" + r.resolveVotes + "/3)");
            h.resolve.setEnabled(true);
        } else {
            h.resolve.setText(votedResolve ? "🌤️ Voted (" + r.resolveVotes + "/3)" : "🌤️ Clear? (" + r.resolveVotes + "/3)");
            h.resolve.setEnabled(!votedResolve);
        }
        h.resolve.setOnClickListener(v -> actions.onVoteResolve(r));

        // Students never see the JPPHB status button
        h.admin.setVisibility(isAdmin ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
        h.admin.setOnClickListener(v -> actions.onAdminStatus(r));
    }

    private static void bindPhoto(@NonNull ImageView imageView, String path) {
        if (path == null || path.isEmpty()) {
            imageView.setTag(null);
            imageView.setImageBitmap(null);
            imageView.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            return;
        }

        imageView.setTag(path);
        imageView.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
        Bitmap cached = PHOTO_CACHE.get(path);
        if (cached != null && !cached.isRecycled()) {
            imageView.setImageBitmap(cached);
            return;
        }

        imageView.setImageBitmap(null);
        int reqWidth = imageView.getResources().getDisplayMetrics().widthPixels;
        int reqHeight = (int) TypedValue.applyDimension(
                TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_DIP, 140, imageView.getResources().getDisplayMetrics());
        WeakReference<ImageView> imageRef = new WeakReference<>(imageView);
        PHOTO_EXECUTOR.execute(() -> {
            Bitmap decoded = decodeSampledPhoto(path, reqWidth, reqHeight);
            if (decoded == null) return;
            PHOTO_CACHE.put(path, decoded);
            ImageView target = imageRef.get();
            if (target != null) {
                target.post(() -> {
                    if (Objects.equals(target.getTag(), path)) target.setImageBitmap(decoded);
                });
            }
        });
    }

    private static Bitmap decodeSampledPhoto(String path, int reqWidth, int reqHeight) {
        BitmapFactory.Options options = new BitmapFactory.Options();
        options.inJustDecodeBounds = true;
        BitmapFactory.decodeFile(path, options);
        if (options.outWidth <= 0 || options.outHeight <= 0) return null;

        int sampleSize = 1;
        while (options.outWidth / (sampleSize * 2) >= reqWidth
                || options.outHeight / (sampleSize * 2) >= reqHeight) {
            sampleSize *= 2;
        }
        options.inSampleSize = sampleSize;
        options.inJustDecodeBounds = false;
        return BitmapFactory.decodeFile(path, options);
    }

    static class CardHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        final TextView header, meta, body, timeline;
        final ImageView photo;
        final Button upvote, resolve, admin;

        CardHolder(Context ctx) {
            super(new LinearLayout(ctx));
            LinearLayout card = (LinearLayout) itemView;
            int pad = dp(ctx, 12);
            card.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
            card.setBackgroundColor(Color.WHITE);
            card.setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad);
            card.setElevation(dp(ctx, 2));
            RecyclerView.LayoutParams lp = new RecyclerView.LayoutParams(
                    ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
            lp.setMargins(0, 0, 0, dp(ctx, 10));
            card.setLayoutParams(lp);

            header = new TextView(ctx);
            header.setTextSize(14f);
            header.setTypeface(null, Typeface.BOLD);
            card.addView(header);

            meta = new TextView(ctx);
            meta.setTextSize(11f);
            meta.setTextColor(Color.rgb(100, 116, 139));
            card.addView(meta);

            body = new TextView(ctx);
            body.setTextSize(12f);
            body.setTextColor(Color.rgb(51, 65, 85));
            body.setPadding(0, dp(ctx, 4), 0, dp(ctx, 4));
            card.addView(body);

            photo = new ImageView(ctx);
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams photoLp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                    ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dp(ctx, 140));
            photoLp.setMargins(0, dp(ctx, 4), 0, dp(ctx, 6));
            photo.setLayoutParams(photoLp);
            photo.setScaleType(ImageView.ScaleType.CENTER_CROP);
            photo.setBackgroundColor(Color.rgb(226, 232, 240));
            photo.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            card.addView(photo);

            timeline = new TextView(ctx);
            timeline.setTextSize(11f);
            timeline.setTextColor(Color.rgb(71, 85, 105));
            timeline.setBackgroundColor(Color.rgb(241, 245, 249));
            timeline.setPadding(dp(ctx, 8), dp(ctx, 6), dp(ctx, 8), dp(ctx, 6));
            card.addView(timeline);

            LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(ctx);
            row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
            row.setPadding(0, dp(ctx, 6), 0, 0);
            upvote = smallButton(ctx);
            resolve = smallButton(ctx);
            admin = smallButton(ctx);
            admin.setText("⚙️ Demo Status");
            row.addView(upvote);
            row.addView(resolve);
            row.addView(admin);
            card.addView(row);
        }

        private static Button smallButton(Context ctx) {
            Button b = new Button(ctx);
            b.setTextSize(11f);
            return b;
        }

        private static int dp(Context ctx, int dp) {
            return (int) TypedValue.applyDimension(
                    TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_DIP, dp, ctx.getResources().getDisplayMetrics());
        }
    }
}

    private static final Pattern COORD_PATTERN = Pattern.compile("\\(([-\\d.]+),\\s*([-\\d.]+)\\)");
    private static final long MAX_REPORT_LOCATION_AGE_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000L;
    private static final long LIVE_GPS_TIMEOUT_MS = 6000L;
    private static final String CAMERA_DIR = "camera";
    private static final String PENDING_PHOTO = "pending_report.jpg";
    private static final int MAX_PHOTO_DIMENSION = 1280;

    private ReportViewModel viewModel;

    private long pendingSubmitRequestId = 0L;
    private long inFlightTriageRequestId = 0L;

    private String latestTriageInputKey = null;

    private Bitmap capturedBitmap = null;
    private Double realGpsLat = null;
    private Double realGpsLon = null;

    private final Handler mainHandler = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());

    /**
     * Full-resolution capture through FileProvider. The old ACTION_IMAGE_CAPTURE "data" extra is only a
     * ~160x120 thumbnail, far too small for photo triage or for a useful report photo (FR 2.1 / 3.1).
     * A fixed file name is used so the callback still works after the fragment is re-created.
     */
    private final ActivityResultLauncher<Uri> takePictureLauncher =
            registerForActivityResult(new ActivityResultContracts.TakePicture(), success -> {
                Context ctx = getContext();
                if (ctx == null) return;
                File photoFile = new File(new File(ctx.getCacheDir(), CAMERA_DIR), PENDING_PHOTO);
                if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(success) && photoFile.exists()) {
                    Bitmap bmp = decodeCapturedPhoto(photoFile, MAX_PHOTO_DIMENSION);
                    if (bmp != null && ivPhotoPreview != null) {
                        capturedBitmap = bmp;
                        viewModel.setCapturedPhoto(bmp);
                        ivPhotoPreview.setImageBitmap(bmp);
                        ivPhotoPreview.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                        requestTriage(false);
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(ctx, "Could not read the captured photo.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    }
                }
                //noinspection ResultOfMethodCallIgnored
                photoFile.delete();
            });

    private final ActivityResultLauncher<String[]> gpsPermLauncher =
            registerForActivityResult(new ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions(), result -> {
                boolean granted = Boolean.TRUE.equals(result.get(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION))
                        || Boolean.TRUE.equals(result.get(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION));
                if (granted) {
                    fetchDeviceGpsCoordinates();
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(getContext(), "GPS permission denied. Using selected campus landmark coordinates.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                }
            });

    private void setupSpinners() {
        String[] locations = {

        spinnerHazardType.setAdapter(new ArrayAdapter<>(
                requireContext(), android.R.layout.simple_spinner_dropdown_item, hazardTypes));
        spinnerLocation.setOnItemSelectedListener(new AdapterView.OnItemSelectedListener() {
            private boolean first = true;   // skip the automatic callback at init
            @Override

                if (first) { first = false; return; }
                realGpsLat = null;
                realGpsLon = null;
                tvGpsStatus.setText("Using landmark coordinates");
