    private void setupButtons(View view) {
        Button btnGps = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_acquire_gps);
        btnPhoto = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_capture_photo);
        btnTriage = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_ai_triage);
        btnSubmit = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_submit_report);
        btnTabAll = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_tab_all_reports);
        btnTabMine = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_tab_my_reports);

        btnGps.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(requireContext(), Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)
                    == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
                    || ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(requireContext(), Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION)
                    == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                fetchDeviceGpsCoordinates();
            } else {
                gpsPermLauncher.launch(new String[]{
                        Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                        Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION});
            }
        });

        btnPhoto.setOnClickListener(v -> launchCamera());

        btnTriage.setOnClickListener(v -> requestTriage(false));

        btnSubmit.setOnClickListener(v -> handleReportSubmission());

        btnTabAll.setOnClickListener(v -> viewModel.setShowOnlyMyReports(false));
        btnTabMine.setOnClickListener(v -> viewModel.setShowOnlyMyReports(true));
    }

    private void launchCamera() {
        try {
            Context ctx = requireContext();
            File dir = new File(ctx.getCacheDir(), CAMERA_DIR);
            if (!dir.exists() && !dir.mkdirs()) {
                Toast.makeText(ctx, "Cannot prepare camera storage.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                return;
            }
            File photoFile = new File(dir, PENDING_PHOTO);
            Uri uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(ctx, ctx.getPackageName() + ".fileprovider", photoFile);
            takePictureLauncher.launch(uri);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Camera not available on this device.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        }
    }

    /** Down-sampled decode + EXIF rotation so the preview, triage upload and saved JPEG are upright and small. */
    @Nullable
    private static Bitmap decodeCapturedPhoto(File file, int maxDimension) {
        String path = file.getAbsolutePath();
        BitmapFactory.Options bounds = new BitmapFactory.Options();
        bounds.inJustDecodeBounds = true;
        BitmapFactory.decodeFile(path, bounds);
        if (bounds.outWidth <= 0 || bounds.outHeight <= 0) return null;

        int sample = 1;
        while (Math.max(bounds.outWidth, bounds.outHeight) / (sample * 2) >= maxDimension) {
            sample *= 2;
        }
        BitmapFactory.Options opts = new BitmapFactory.Options();
        opts.inSampleSize = sample;
        Bitmap bmp = BitmapFactory.decodeFile(path, opts);
        if (bmp == null) return null;

        int rotation = 0;
        try {
            int orientation = new ExifInterface(path).getAttributeInt(
                    ExifInterface.TAG_ORIENTATION, ExifInterface.ORIENTATION_NORMAL);
            if (orientation == ExifInterface.ORIENTATION_ROTATE_90) rotation = 90;
            else if (orientation == ExifInterface.ORIENTATION_ROTATE_180) rotation = 180;
            else if (orientation == ExifInterface.ORIENTATION_ROTATE_270) rotation = 270;
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        if (rotation != 0) {
            Matrix m = new Matrix();
            m.postRotate(rotation);
            Bitmap rotated = Bitmap.createBitmap(bmp, 0, 0, bmp.getWidth(), bmp.getHeight(), m, true);
            if (rotated != bmp) bmp.recycle();
            bmp = rotated;
        }
        return bmp;
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private void fetchDeviceGpsCoordinates() {
        LocationManager lm = (LocationManager) requireContext().getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE);
        Location loc = null;
        if (lm != null) {
            for (String p : new String[]{LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER,
                    LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER, LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER}) {
                try {
                    Location l = lm.getLastKnownLocation(p);
                    if (l != null && (loc == null || l.getTime() > loc.getTime())) loc = l;
                } catch (SecurityException | IllegalArgumentException ignored) {}
            }
        }
        boolean staleFix = loc != null
                && (System.currentTimeMillis() - loc.getTime() > MAX_REPORT_LOCATION_AGE_MS);
        if (loc != null && !staleFix) {
            applyGpsFix(loc, false);
            return;
        }

        if (lm != null) {
            String activeProvider = null;
            for (String p : new String[]{LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER, LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER}) {
                try {
                    if (lm.isProviderEnabled(p)) {
                        activeProvider = p;
                        break;
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
            if (activeProvider != null) {
                if (gpsCancelSignal != null) {
                    gpsCancelSignal.cancel();
                }
                final CancellationSignal signal = new CancellationSignal();
                gpsCancelSignal = signal;
                final boolean wasStale = staleFix;
                tvGpsStatus.setText("📡 Acquiring live GPS fix...");

                Runnable timeoutTask = () -> {
                    if (!signal.isCanceled()) {
                        signal.cancel();
                        if (isAdded() && tvGpsStatus != null) {
                            realGpsLat = null;
                            realGpsLon = null;
                            tvGpsStatus.setText(wasStale
                                    ? "⚠️ Location fix is over 10 minutes old & live GPS timed out; using landmark coordinates."
                                    : "⚠️ Indoor/No live GPS signal; fallback to selected landmark coordinates.");
                        }
                    }
                };
                mainHandler.postDelayed(timeoutTask, LIVE_GPS_TIMEOUT_MS);

                try {
                    LocationManagerCompat.getCurrentLocation(
                            lm,
                            activeProvider,
                            signal,
                            ContextCompat.getMainExecutor(requireContext()),
                            freshLocation -> {
                                mainHandler.removeCallbacks(timeoutTask);
                                if (!isAdded() || tvGpsStatus == null) return;
                                if (freshLocation != null) {
                                    applyGpsFix(freshLocation, true);
                                } else {
                                    realGpsLat = null;
                                    realGpsLon = null;
                                    tvGpsStatus.setText(wasStale
                                            ? "⚠️ Location fix is over 10 minutes old; using selected landmark coordinates."
                                            : "⚠️ Indoor/No GPS signal; fallback to selected landmark coordinates.");
                                }
                            }
                    );
                    return;
                } catch (SecurityException | IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                    mainHandler.removeCallbacks(timeoutTask);
                }
            }
        }

        realGpsLat = null;
        realGpsLon = null;
        tvGpsStatus.setText(staleFix
                ? "⚠️ Location fix is over 10 minutes old; using selected landmark coordinates."
                : "⚠️ Indoor/No GPS signal; fallback to selected landmark coordinates.");
    }

    private void applyGpsFix(@NonNull Location loc, boolean isLiveFix) {
        realGpsLat = loc.getLatitude();
        realGpsLon = loc.getLongitude();
        long ageSeconds = Math.max(0L, (System.currentTimeMillis() - loc.getTime()) / 1000L);
        if (isLiveFix) {
            tvGpsStatus.setText(String.format(Locale.US,
                    "✅ Live GPS fix acquired: (%.5f, %.5f)", realGpsLat, realGpsLon));
        } else {
            tvGpsStatus.setText(String.format(Locale.US,
                    "✅ Last known location (%ds old): (%.5f, %.5f)", ageSeconds, realGpsLat, realGpsLon));
        }
    }

    private void observeData() {
        viewModel.getTriageResult().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), res -> {
            if (res != null && res.requestId == inFlightTriageRequestId) {
                inFlightTriageRequestId = 0L;
                inFlightTriageInputKey = null;
            }
            if (res == null || res.requestId != latestTriageRequestId) return;
            if (!res.inputKey.equals(currentTriageInputKey())) {
                latestTriage = null;
                latestTriageInputKey = null;
                tvTriageResult.setText("Form changed during analysis. Review the form and submit again.");
                if (submitAfterTriage && pendingSubmitRequestId == res.requestId) {
                    finishPendingSubmission();
                    Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Form changed; please submit again.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                }
                return;
            }

            latestTriage = res.result;
            latestTriageInputKey = res.inputKey;
            if (res.result != null) {
                tvTriageResult.setText(String.format(Locale.US,
                        "Suggested type: %s\nSeverity: %s (Confidence: %.0f%%)\n%s",
                        res.result.detectedType, res.result.severity,
                        res.result.confidence * 100, res.result.note));
                if (submitAfterTriage && pendingSubmitRequestId == res.requestId) {
                    finishPendingSubmission();
                    handleReportSubmission();
                }
            }
        });

        viewModel.getReports().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), reports -> refreshReportList());
        viewModel.getUserRole().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), role -> refreshReportList());
        viewModel.getShowOnlyMyReports().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), onlyMine -> {
            updateTabFilterHighlight(Boolean.TRUE.equals(onlyMine));
            refreshReportList();
        });
    }

    private void updateTabFilterHighlight(boolean onlyMine) {
        if (btnTabAll != null && btnTabMine != null) {
            btnTabAll.setAlpha(onlyMine ? 0.55f : 1.0f);
            btnTabMine.setAlpha(onlyMine ? 1.0f : 0.55f);
        }
    }

    private String currentTriageInputKey() {
        String title = etTitle.getText().toString().trim();
        int hazardType = spinnerHazardType.getSelectedItemPosition();
        int photoIdentity = capturedBitmap != null ? System.identityHashCode(capturedBitmap) : 0;
        return title.length() + ":" + title + "|" + hazardType + "|" + photoIdentity;
    }

    private void requestTriage(boolean submitWhenComplete) {
        String inputKey = currentTriageInputKey();
        if (inFlightTriageRequestId != 0L && inputKey.equals(inFlightTriageInputKey)) {
            if (submitWhenComplete) {
                submitAfterTriage = true;
                pendingSubmitRequestId = inFlightTriageRequestId;
                btnSubmit.setEnabled(false);
                if (btnPhoto != null) btnPhoto.setEnabled(false);
                if (btnTriage != null) btnTriage.setEnabled(false);
            }
            return;
        }
        latestTriage = null;
        latestTriageInputKey = null;
        long requestId = viewModel.performVisionTriage(
                capturedBitmap,
                spinnerHazardType.getSelectedItemPosition(),
                etTitle.getText().toString().trim(),
                inputKey);
        latestTriageRequestId = requestId;
        inFlightTriageRequestId = requestId;
        inFlightTriageInputKey = inputKey;
        if (submitWhenComplete) {
            submitAfterTriage = true;
            pendingSubmitRequestId = requestId;
            btnSubmit.setEnabled(false);
            if (btnPhoto != null) btnPhoto.setEnabled(false);
            if (btnTriage != null) btnTriage.setEnabled(false);
        }
        tvTriageResult.setText("Analyzing the current form in the background…");
    }

    private void finishPendingSubmission() {
        submitAfterTriage = false;
        pendingSubmitRequestId = 0L;
        if (btnSubmit != null) btnSubmit.setEnabled(true);
        if (btnPhoto != null) btnPhoto.setEnabled(true);
        if (btnTriage != null) btnTriage.setEnabled(true);
    }

    private void handleReportSubmission() {
        String title = etTitle.getText().toString().trim();
        String desc = etDesc.getText().toString().trim();
        if (title.isEmpty()) {
            Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Please enter a short hazard title.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            return;
        }
        if (desc.isEmpty()) {
            desc = "Reported via UM-ClearFlow mobile client.";
        }

        String locationStr = spinnerLocation.getSelectedItem().toString();
        String hazardType = spinnerHazardType.getSelectedItem().toString();

        // Prefer real GPS; otherwise parse the chosen landmark's coordinates. Never random.
        double lat = 3.1281, lon = 101.6505;
        if (realGpsLat != null && realGpsLon != null) {
            lat = realGpsLat;
            lon = realGpsLon;
        } else {
            Matcher m = COORD_PATTERN.matcher(locationStr);
            if (m.find()) {
                lat = Double.parseDouble(m.group(1));
                lon = Double.parseDouble(m.group(2));
            }
        }

        String currentInputKey = currentTriageInputKey();
        if (latestTriage == null || !currentInputKey.equals(latestTriageInputKey)) {
            requestTriage(true);
            return;
        }

        String id = "CF-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
        // Include the date: "Today, HH:mm" became wrong as soon as the app was reopened the next day
        String timeStr = new SimpleDateFormat("dd MMM, HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(new Date());
        String myEmail = CampusRepository.getInstance()
                .getProfileField("siswa_email", "demo.student@siswa.um.edu.my");
        String timeline = timeStr + " • Submitted"
                + (capturedBitmap != null ? " (With Photo Estimate)" : " (Text Report)");

        String photoUri = "";
        if (capturedBitmap != null) {
            photoUri = CampusRepository.getInstance().saveReportPhotoToInternalStorage(id, capturedBitmap);
        }

        HazardReport newReport = new HazardReport(
                id, title, desc, locationStr, hazardType,
                latestTriage.severity, "Submitted",
                lat, lon, timeStr,
                1, 0, latestTriage.confidence, latestTriage.note,
                myEmail, timeline, photoUri
        );

        viewModel.submitNewReport(newReport);

        etTitle.setText("");
        etDesc.setText("");
        capturedBitmap = null;
        viewModel.setCapturedPhoto(null);
        latestTriage = null;
        ivPhotoPreview.setVisibility(View.GONE);
        tvTriageResult.setText("Report saved on this device and queued for sync to the ClearFlow server. "
                + "JPPHB dispatch is not connected. Nearby reports of the same type (<50 m) are merged.");
        realGpsLat = null;
        realGpsLon = null;
        tvGpsStatus.setText("Using landmark coordinates");
        viewModel.clearTriage();
        Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Report saved; syncing…", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
    }

    private void refreshReportList() {
        if (reportAdapter == null) return;
        List<HazardReport> list = viewModel.getReports().getValue();
        if (list == null) return;

        boolean onlyMine = Boolean.TRUE.equals(viewModel.getShowOnlyMyReports().getValue());
        String myEmail = CampusRepository.getInstance()
                .getProfileField("siswa_email", "demo.student@siswa.um.edu.my");

        List<HazardReport> visible = new ArrayList<>(list.size());
        for (HazardReport r : list) {
            if (!onlyMine || myEmail.equalsIgnoreCase(r.reporterEmail)) visible.add(r);
        }
        reportAdapter.setAdmin(CampusRepository.getInstance().isAdmin());
        reportAdapter.submitList(visible);
    }

    private void showStatusUpdateDialog(HazardReport report) {
        if (!CampusRepository.getInstance().isAdmin()) {
            Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Admin role required!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            return;
        }
        String[] statuses = {"Submitted", "Verified", "In Progress - JPPHB", "Resolved"};
        new AlertDialog.Builder(requireContext())
                .setTitle("Update Local Demo Status (" + report.id + ")")
                .setItems(statuses, (dialog, which) -> viewModel.updateStatus(report.id, statuses[which]))
                .setNegativeButton("Cancel", null)
                .show();
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        if (gpsCancelSignal != null) {
            gpsCancelSignal.cancel();
            gpsCancelSignal = null;
        }
        mainHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
        super.onDestroyView();
    }
}

package com.um.clearflow;

import android.graphics.Bitmap;

import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData;
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData;
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

public class ReportViewModel extends ViewModel {

    public static class TriageSubmissionResult {
        public final long requestId;
        public final String inputKey;
        public final CampusRepository.TriageResult result;

        public TriageSubmissionResult(long requestId, String inputKey, CampusRepository.TriageResult result) {
            this.requestId = requestId;
            this.inputKey = inputKey != null ? inputKey : "";
            this.result = result;
        }
    }

    private final CampusRepository repository = CampusRepository.getInstance();
    private final ExecutorService triageExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
    private final AtomicLong requestIdGenerator = new AtomicLong(0L);
    private final MutableLiveData<TriageSubmissionResult> triageResultLiveData = new MutableLiveData<>();
    private final MutableLiveData<Boolean> showOnlyMyReports = new MutableLiveData<>(false);
    // Survives rotation / Fragment view recreation (Fragment fields do not)
    private Bitmap capturedPhoto;

    public Bitmap getCapturedPhoto() {
        return capturedPhoto;
    }

    public void setCapturedPhoto(Bitmap photo) {
        capturedPhoto = photo;
    }

    public LiveData<List<HazardReport>> getReports() {
        return repository.getReportsLiveData();
    }

    public LiveData<String> getUserRole() {
        return repository.getUserRoleLiveData();
    }

    public LiveData<TriageSubmissionResult> getTriageResult() {
        return triageResultLiveData;
    }

    public LiveData<Boolean> getShowOnlyMyReports() {
        return showOnlyMyReports;
    }

    public void setShowOnlyMyReports(boolean onlyMine) {
        showOnlyMyReports.setValue(onlyMine);
    }

    public long performVisionTriage(Bitmap photo, int hazardTypeIndex, String title, String inputKey) {
        final long reqId = requestIdGenerator.incrementAndGet();
        final String key = inputKey != null ? inputKey : "";
        // Run vision triage on background thread so FastAPI /api/v1/triage can be queried first
        triageExecutor.execute(() -> {
            CampusRepository.TriageResult res = repository.runVisionTriage(photo, hazardTypeIndex, title);
            triageResultLiveData.postValue(new TriageSubmissionResult(reqId, key, res));
        });
        return reqId;
    }

    public long performVisionTriage(Bitmap photo, int hazardTypeIndex, String title) {
        return performVisionTriage(photo, hazardTypeIndex, title, "");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onCleared() {
        triageExecutor.shutdownNow();
        super.onCleared();
    }

    public void submitNewReport(HazardReport report) {
        repository.addReport(report);
    }

    public void upvote(String id) {
        repository.upvoteReport(id);
    }

    public void voteResolve(String id) {
        repository.voteResolveReport(id);
    }

    public void updateStatus(String id, String status) {
        repository.updateReportStatus(id, status);
    }

    public void clearTriage() {
        triageResultLiveData.setValue(null);
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\SyncClient.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import org.json.JSONException;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.DataOutputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.URL;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

/**
 * Plain HttpURLConnection client for the FastAPI report-sync endpoints. Every call blocks, so it must only be
 * used from a background thread. Never throws: network failure is reported as Response.code == -1.
 */
public final class SyncClient {

    public static final int NETWORK_ERROR = -1;
    private static final int MAX_DOWNLOAD_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024;

    public static final class Response {
        public final int code;
        public final JSONObject json; // may be null (empty or non-JSON body)

        Response(int code, JSONObject json) {
            this.code = code;
            this.json = json;
        }

        public boolean ok() {
            return code >= 200 && code < 300;
        }
    }

    /** One report as returned by the server, plus per-device flags. */
    public static final class Remote {
        public final HazardReport report;
        public final boolean hasPhoto;
        public final boolean myUpvote;
        public final boolean myResolve;

        Remote(HazardReport report, boolean hasPhoto, boolean myUpvote, boolean myResolve) {
            this.report = report;
            this.hasPhoto = hasPhoto;
            this.myUpvote = myUpvote;
            this.myResolve = myResolve;
        }
    }

    private final String baseUrl;

    public SyncClient(String baseUrl) {
        this.baseUrl = baseUrl.endsWith("/") ? baseUrl : baseUrl + "/";
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ requests

    public Response get(String path) {
        HttpURLConnection conn = null;
        try {
            conn = open(path, "GET");
            return read(conn);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return new Response(NETWORK_ERROR, null);
        } finally {
            if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
        }
    }

    public Response postJson(String path, JSONObject body, String bearerToken) {
        HttpURLConnection conn = null;
        try {
            conn = open(path, "POST");
            conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=UTF-8");
            if (bearerToken != null) conn.setRequestProperty("Authorization", "Bearer " + bearerToken);
            conn.setDoOutput(true);
            conn.getOutputStream().write((body != null ? body.toString() : "{}").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            return read(conn);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return new Response(NETWORK_ERROR, null);
        } finally {
            if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
        }
    }

    public Response uploadPhoto(String path, File file, String deviceId) {
        HttpURLConnection conn = null;
        try {
            byte[] jpeg = readFile(file);
            String boundary = "----UMClearFlowSync" + System.currentTimeMillis();
            conn = open(path, "POST");
            conn.setReadTimeout(20000);
            conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "multipart/form-data; boundary=" + boundary);
            conn.setDoOutput(true);
            try (DataOutputStream out = new DataOutputStream(conn.getOutputStream())) {
                out.writeBytes("--" + boundary + "\r\n");
                out.writeBytes("Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"device_id\"\r\n\r\n");
                out.write(deviceId.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                out.writeBytes("\r\n--" + boundary + "\r\n");
                out.writeBytes("Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"image\"; filename=\"hazard.jpg\"\r\n");
                out.writeBytes("Content-Type: image/jpeg\r\n\r\n");
                out.write(jpeg);
                out.writeBytes("\r\n--" + boundary + "--\r\n");
            }
            return read(conn);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return new Response(NETWORK_ERROR, null);
        } finally {
            if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
        }
    }

    /** Returns the bytes, or null if the photo does not exist or the request failed. */
    public byte[] download(String path) {
        HttpURLConnection conn = null;
        try {
            conn = open(path, "GET");
            conn.setReadTimeout(20000);
            if (conn.getResponseCode() != HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) return null;
            try (InputStream in = conn.getInputStream()) {
                ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
                int n;
                while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) {
                    out.write(buf, 0, n);
                    if (out.size() > MAX_DOWNLOAD_BYTES) return null;
                }
                return out.toByteArray();
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            return null;
        } finally {
            if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
        }
    }

    /** Exchanges the staff PIN for a short-lived bearer token; null on any failure. */
    public String adminLogin(String pin) {
        try {
            Response r = postJson("api/v1/admin/login", new JSONObject().put("pin", pin), null);
            if (r.ok() && r.json != null && r.json.has("token")) return r.json.getString("token");
        } catch (JSONException ignored) {}
        return null;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ JSON mapping

    public static JSONObject reportToJson(HazardReport r, String deviceId) throws JSONException {
        return new JSONObject()
                .put("id", r.id)
                .put("title", r.title)
                .put("description", r.description != null ? r.description : "")
                .put("location_name", r.locationName != null ? r.locationName : "")
                .put("hazard_type", r.hazardType)
                .put("severity", r.severity)
                .put("latitude", r.latitude)
                .put("longitude", r.longitude)
                .put("timestamp", r.timestamp != null ? r.timestamp : "")
                .put("ai_confidence", Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, r.aiConfidence)))
                .put("ai_triage_note", r.aiTriageNote != null ? r.aiTriageNote : "")
                .put("reporter_email", r.reporterEmail != null ? r.reporterEmail : "")
                .put("timeline", r.timeline != null ? r.timeline : "")
                .put("device_id", deviceId);
    }

    public static Remote parseRemote(JSONObject o) {
        HazardReport r = new HazardReport(
                o.optString("id", ""),
                o.optString("title", ""),
                o.optString("description", ""),
                o.optString("location_name", ""),
                o.optString("hazard_type", "Flash Flood"),
                o.optString("severity", "Medium"),
                o.optString("status", "Submitted"),
                o.optDouble("latitude", 0.0),
                o.optDouble("longitude", 0.0),
                o.optString("timestamp", ""),
                o.optInt("upvotes", 0),
                o.optInt("resolve_votes", 0),
                o.optDouble("ai_confidence", 0.0),
                o.optString("ai_triage_note", ""),
                o.optString("reporter_email", ""),
                o.optString("timeline", ""),
                "");
        long created = o.optLong("created_at", 0L);
        if (created > 0) r.createdAt = created;
        return new Remote(r, o.optBoolean("has_photo", false),
                o.optBoolean("my_upvote", false), o.optBoolean("my_resolve", false));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ internals

    private HttpURLConnection open(String path, String method) throws IOException {
        HttpURLConnection conn = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(baseUrl + path).openConnection();
        conn.setRequestMethod(method);
        conn.setConnectTimeout(3000);
        conn.setReadTimeout(8000);
        return conn;
    }

    private static Response read(HttpURLConnection conn) throws IOException {
        int code = conn.getResponseCode();
        InputStream in = code >= 400 ? conn.getErrorStream() : conn.getInputStream();
        String body = "";
        if (in != null) {
            try (BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
                StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                String line;
                while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) sb.append(line);
                body = sb.toString();
            }
        }
        JSONObject json = null;
        if (!body.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                json = new JSONObject(body);
            } catch (JSONException ignored) {}
        }
        return new Response(code, json);
    }

    private static byte[] readFile(File f) throws IOException {
        try (FileInputStream in = new FileInputStream(f)) {
            ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
            int n;
            while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) out.write(buf, 0, n);
            return out.toByteArray();
        }
    }
}

package com.um.clearflow;

import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Button;
import android.widget.CheckBox;
import android.widget.EditText;
import android.widget.TextView;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.Locale;

public class WeatherAiFragment extends Fragment {

    private WeatherAiViewModel viewModel;
    private TextView tvWeatherLive;
    private TextView tvRiskMatrix;
    private TextView tvChatHistory;
    private TextView tvBgAlertStatus;
    private CheckBox cbBgWeatherAlerts;
    private EditText etChatInput;
    private AlertPrefs alertPrefs;

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater, @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        View view = inflater.inflate(R.layout.fragment_weather_ai, container, false);
        viewModel = new ViewModelProvider(requireActivity()).get(WeatherAiViewModel.class);
        alertPrefs = new AlertPrefs(requireContext());

        tvWeatherLive = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_weather_live);
        tvRiskMatrix = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_risk_matrix);
        tvChatHistory = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_chat_history);
        tvBgAlertStatus = view.findViewById(R.id.tv_bg_alert_status);
        cbBgWeatherAlerts = view.findViewById(R.id.cb_bg_weather_alerts);
        etChatInput = view.findViewById(R.id.et_chat_input);

        cbBgWeatherAlerts.setChecked(alertPrefs.isEnabled());
        refreshBgAlertStatusText();
        cbBgWeatherAlerts.setOnCheckedChangeListener((buttonView, isChecked) -> {
            alertPrefs.setEnabled(isChecked);
            WeatherAlertScheduler.apply(requireContext());
            if (isChecked) {
                WeatherAlertScheduler.runNow(requireContext(), false);
            }
            refreshBgAlertStatusText();
        });

        Button btnRefresh = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_refresh_weather);
        Button btnSimSquall = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_simulate_squall);
        if (!BuildConfig.DEBUG) {
            btnSimSquall.setVisibility(View.GONE);
        }
        Button btnPromptShelter = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_prompt_shelter);
        Button btnPromptRoute = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_prompt_route);
        Button btnPromptSos = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_prompt_sos);
        Button btnSendChat = view.findViewById(R.id.btn_send_chat);

        btnRefresh.setOnClickListener(v -> viewModel.fetchLiveWeatherAndForecast(false));
        btnSimSquall.setOnClickListener(v -> viewModel.fetchLiveWeatherAndForecast(true));

        btnPromptShelter.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            if (!viewModel.sendUserChatPrompt("Where are the nearest flood evacuation shelters?")) {
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Please wait for the current reply...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            }
        });
        btnPromptRoute.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            if (!viewModel.sendUserChatPrompt("Is the route from Pantai Gate to FCSIT safe right now?")) {
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Please wait for the current reply...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            }
        });
        btnPromptSos.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            if (!viewModel.sendUserChatPrompt("What are the emergency SOS contact numbers?")) {
                Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Please wait for the current reply...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            }
        });

        btnSendChat.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            String msg = etChatInput.getText().toString().trim();
            if (!msg.isEmpty()) {
                if (viewModel.sendUserChatPrompt(msg)) {
                    etChatInput.setText("");
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(getContext(), "Please wait for the current reply...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                }
            }
        });

        viewModel.getWeatherSummary().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), text -> {
            tvWeatherLive.setText(text);
            refreshBgAlertStatusText();
        });
        viewModel.getRiskMatrix().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), text -> tvRiskMatrix.setText(text));
        viewModel.getChatHistory().observe(getViewLifecycleOwner(), text -> tvChatHistory.setText(text));

        // 监听工单列表变化，实时重算区域风险评分矩阵（解决冷启动异步加载与新增/核销工单后风险分不刷新的问题）
        CampusRepository.getInstance().getReportsLiveData().observe(
                getViewLifecycleOwner(), reports -> viewModel.recomputeRiskFromLatestReports());

        // 首次进入拉取一次真实 Open-Meteo 6 小时预报
        viewModel.fetchLiveWeatherAndForecast(false);

        return view;
    }

    @Override
    public void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        refreshBgAlertStatusText();
    }

    private void refreshBgAlertStatusText() {
        if (tvBgAlertStatus == null || alertPrefs == null) return;
        if (!alertPrefs.isEnabled()) {
            tvBgAlertStatus.setText("Background weather checks paused by user.");
            return;
        }
        long lastMs = alertPrefs.lastCheckMs();
        String summary = alertPrefs.lastSummary();
        if (lastMs <= 0L) {
            tvBgAlertStatus.setText("Background check: scheduled (~15 min) • " + summary);
        } else {
            String timeStr = new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss", Locale.US).format(new Date(lastMs));
            tvBgAlertStatus.setText("Last check " + timeStr + " • " + summary);
        }
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\WeatherAiViewModel.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import android.content.Context;

import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.URL;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData;
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData;
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel;

public class WeatherAiViewModel extends ViewModel {

    private final CampusRepository repository = CampusRepository.getInstance();
    private final ExecutorService executor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();

    private volatile boolean chatPending = false;
    private volatile int lastMaxProb = 50;

    private final MutableLiveData<String> weatherSummaryLiveData = new MutableLiveData<>(
            "Fetching live Open-Meteo 6-hour forecast for Universiti Malaya (3.1245, 101.6525)...");
    private final MutableLiveData<String> riskMatrixLiveData = new MutableLiveData<>(
            "Calculating Forecast × Historical Flood Weight fusion score...");
    private final MutableLiveData<String> chatHistoryLiveData = new MutableLiveData<>(
            "🤖 UM-ClearFlow Assistant: Hello! Ask me about flood-safe routes, nearest shelters, active hazards, or monsoon SOPs.\n" +
                    "⚠️ Disclaimer: In life-threatening situations, always call " + EmergencyContacts.NATIONAL_EMERGENCY +
                    " or UM Security Office (" + EmergencyContacts.UM_SECURITY_DISPLAY + ") first.");

    @Override
    protected void onCleared() {
        executor.shutdownNow();
        super.onCleared();
    }

    public LiveData<String> getWeatherSummary() {
        return weatherSummaryLiveData;
    }

    public LiveData<String> getRiskMatrix() {
        return riskMatrixLiveData;
    }

    public LiveData<String> getChatHistory() {
        return chatHistoryLiveData;
    }

    public void recomputeRiskFromLatestReports() {
        computeZoneRiskFusion(repository.getLatestRainfallMm(), repository.getForecast3hRainMm(), lastMaxProb);
    }

    // FR 3.2 / 5.3: current weather + next 6 hours. Parsing and alert rules are shared with the background worker.
    public void fetchLiveWeatherAndForecast(boolean simulateSquallForDemo) {
        executor.execute(() -> {
            WeatherSnapshot s;
            if (simulateSquallForDemo) {
                s = WeatherSnapshot.simulatedSquall();
            } else {
                try {
                    s = WeatherService.fetch();
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    double fallbackRain = repository.getLatestRainfallMm();
                    lastMaxProb = 50;
                    weatherSummaryLiveData.postValue(
                            "⚠️ Offline / Network Timeout reaching Open-Meteo.\n" +
                                    "Using cached rainfall (" + fallbackRain + " mm) & SQLite hazard weights for risk estimation.");
                    computeZoneRiskFusion(fallbackRain, fallbackRain * 2.0, 50);
                    return;
                }
            }

            lastMaxProb = s.maxProbPct;
            repository.updateWeatherMetrics(s.currentRainMm, s.next3hRainMm);

            if (simulateSquallForDemo) {
                weatherSummaryLiveData.postValue(String.format(Locale.US,
                        "🌧️ [SIMULATED MONSOON SQUALL - DEMO MODE]\n" +
                                "• Current Rainfall: %.1f mm/h | Temp: %.1f°C | Wind: %.1f km/h\n" +
                                "• Next 3-Hour Cumulative Forecast: %.1f mm (%d%% Probability)\n" +
                                "• Demo thresholds exceeded; this is simulated data, not a real warning.\n" +
                                "📌 FR 5.3 Multi-Hazard Tip: %s",
                        s.currentRainMm, s.tempC, s.windKmh, s.next3hRainMm, s.maxProbPct,
                        WeatherService.buildResilienceTip(s.tempC, s.currentRainMm, s.next3hRainMm, s.windKmh)));
            } else {
                weatherSummaryLiveData.postValue(String.format(Locale.US,
                        "⛅ Live Open-Meteo Telemetry (UM Campus 3.1245°N, 101.6525°E)\n" +
                                "• Current: %.1f°C | Rain: %.1f mm | Wind: %.1f km/h\n" +
                                "• Next 3h Forecast Sum: %.1f mm | Next 6h Sum: %.1f mm\n" +
                                "• Max Rain Probability (6h): %d%% (Alert Threshold: 15.0 mm/3h)\n" +
                                "📌 FR 5.3 Resilience Tip: %s",
                        s.tempC, s.currentRainMm, s.windKmh, s.next3hRainMm, s.next6hRainMm, s.maxProbPct,
                        WeatherService.buildResilienceTip(s.tempC, s.currentRainMm, s.next3hRainMm, s.windKmh)));
            }

            computeZoneRiskFusion(s.currentRainMm, s.next3hRainMm, s.maxProbPct);

            // Shares its cooldown with the background worker: the same alert is never shown twice.
            Context appContext = repository.getAppContext();
            if (appContext != null) {
                WeatherAlertDispatcher.dispatch(appContext, s, simulateSquallForDemo);
            }
        });
    }

    // FR 3.2: rainfall forecast × zone topographic weight × active hazards × historical report density
    private void computeZoneRiskFusion(double currentRain, double next3hRain, int maxProb) {
        List<HazardReport> reports = repository.getReportsLiveData().getValue();
        int[] active = new int[3];   // 0 = Pantai/Budi, 1 = FCSIT/Library, 2 = KK12
        int[] history = new int[3];  // all reports ever recorded in the zone, incl. resolved

        if (reports != null) {
            for (HazardReport r : reports) {
                String loc = r.locationName != null ? r.locationName : "";
                int zone;
                if (loc.contains("Pantai") || loc.contains("Budi")) zone = 0;
                else if (loc.contains("FCSIT") || loc.contains("Library")) zone = 1;
                else if (loc.contains("KK12")) zone = 2;
                else continue;
                history[zone]++;
                if (!"Resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(r.status)) {
                    active[zone] += "Critical".equalsIgnoreCase(r.severity) ? 2 : 1;
                }
            }
        }

        double baseRainFactor = currentRain * 2.2 + next3hRain * 1.4 + (maxProb * 0.12);
        double[] topo = {1.45, 1.15, 1.05};
        int[] penalty = {15, 14, 12};
        int[] score = new int[3];
        for (int z = 0; z < 3; z++) {
            double historyFactor = 1.0 + Math.min(0.40, history[z] * 0.04); // +4% per past report, max +40%
            score[z] = (int) Math.min(100, Math.round(baseRainFactor * topo[z] * historyFactor + active[z] * penalty[z]));
        }

        String matrixText = String.format(Locale.US,
                "Illustrative rule-based risk estimate (unvalidated formula: weather forecast × zone weight × history + active reports):\n" +
                        "1. Lingkungan Budi / Pantai Gate (Weight 1.45x, %d past reports): %d/100 [%s]\n" +
                        "2. FCSIT & Central Library Culvert (Weight 1.15x, %d past reports): %d/100 [%s]\n" +
                        "3. KK12 Residential Corridor (Weight 1.05x, %d past reports): %d/100 [%s]",
                history[0], score[0], levelFromScore(score[0]),
                history[1], score[1], levelFromScore(score[1]),
                history[2], score[2], levelFromScore(score[2]));

        riskMatrixLiveData.postValue(matrixText);
    }

    private String levelFromScore(int score) {
        if (score >= 70) return "CRITICAL RISK 🔴";
        if (score >= 40) return "MODERATE RISK 🟠";
        return "LOW RISK 🟢";
    }

    // FR 3.3: 真实用户提问输入 -> 优先请求后端 FastAPI LLM (/api/v1/chat)，离线/无后端时自动使用本地 RAG 状态引擎
    public boolean sendUserChatPrompt(String userPrompt) {
        if (userPrompt == null || userPrompt.trim().isEmpty()) return false;
        // Reject new prompts while a reply is pending so caller does not clear the input box prematurely
        if (chatPending) return false;
        chatPending = true;
        final String cleanPrompt = userPrompt.trim();

        String currentHistory = chatHistoryLiveData.getValue();
        final String updatedWithUser = (currentHistory != null ? currentHistory + "\n\n" : "")
                + "🧑 You: " + cleanPrompt;
        chatHistoryLiveData.setValue(updatedWithUser + "\n🤖 Assistant: Thinking...");

        executor.execute(() -> {
            try {
                String reply = tryBackendChatOrContextualFallback(cleanPrompt);
                chatHistoryLiveData.postValue(updatedWithUser + "\n🤖 Assistant: " + reply);
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                chatHistoryLiveData.postValue(updatedWithUser
                        + "\n🤖 Assistant: I couldn't prepare a response. Please try again or use the campus emergency contacts.");
            } finally {
                chatPending = false;
            }
        });
        return true;
    }

    private String buildActiveHazardsSummary() {
        List<HazardReport> reports = repository.getReportsLiveData().getValue();
        if (reports == null || reports.isEmpty()) return "";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (HazardReport r : reports) {
            if (!"Resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(r.status)) {
                sb.append("• [").append(r.severity).append("] ")
                        .append(r.title).append(" at ").append(r.locationName).append("\n");
            }
        }
        return sb.toString().trim();
    }

    private String tryBackendChatOrContextualFallback(String prompt) {
        // 1. 尝试调用本地 FastAPI 后端 (http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/chat)，绝不在 APK 内硬编码任何第三方 API Key
        HttpURLConnection conn = null;
        try {
            URL url = new URL(CampusRepository.API_BASE_URL + "api/v1/chat");
            conn = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
            conn.setRequestMethod("POST");
            conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=UTF-8");
            conn.setConnectTimeout(3000);
            conn.setReadTimeout(20000);   // an LLM reply can take several seconds
            conn.setDoOutput(true);

            JSONObject body = new JSONObject();
            body.put("prompt", prompt);
            body.put("rainfall_mm", repository.getLatestRainfallMm());
            body.put("forecast_3h_mm", repository.getForecast3hRainMm());
            body.put("mobility_mode", repository.getProfileField("mobility_pref", "Walking (Pedestrian / Covered Walkway)"));
            body.put("active_hazards_summary", buildActiveHazardsSummary());

            try (OutputStream os = conn.getOutputStream()) {
                os.write(body.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            }

            if (conn.getResponseCode() == 200) {
                BufferedReader br = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(conn.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                StringBuilder resp = new StringBuilder();
                String line;
                while ((line = br.readLine()) != null) resp.append(line);
                br.close();
                JSONObject json = new JSONObject(resp.toString());
                if (json.has("reply")) {
                    return json.getString("reply")
                            + "\n⚠️ [Emergency Priority: Dial " + EmergencyContacts.NATIONAL_EMERGENCY
                            + " or UM Security Office " + EmergencyContacts.UM_SECURITY_DISPLAY + " if trapped]";
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            // 本地 FastAPI 未启动时，无缝使用端侧实时 SQLite 上下文引擎回答
        } finally {
            if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
        }

        // 2. 端侧实时数据感知问答引擎（读取当前 SQLite 隐患、降雨预报、避难所与用户出行偏好）
        String lower = prompt.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        List<HazardReport> reports = repository.getReportsLiveData().getValue();
        int activeCount = 0;
        StringBuilder activeLocations = new StringBuilder();
        if (reports != null) {
            for (HazardReport r : reports) {
                if (!"Resolved".equalsIgnoreCase(r.status)) {
                    activeCount++;
                    // Titles are free text from other users since reports are synced: show severity + place only
                    activeLocations.append("• [").append(r.severity).append("] ")
                            .append(r.locationName).append("\n");
                }
            }
        }

        String mobility = repository.getProfileField("mobility_pref", "Walking (Pedestrian / Covered Walkway)");
        double rain = repository.getLatestRainfallMm();
        double forecast3h = repository.getForecast3hRainMm();

        StringBuilder answer = new StringBuilder();
        boolean urgent = lower.contains("trapped") || lower.contains("drown") || lower.contains("injur")
                || lower.contains("bleeding") || lower.contains("unconscious") || lower.contains("help me")
                || lower.contains("emergency") || lower.contains("tolong") || lower.contains("terperangkap")
                || lower.contains("kecemasan") || lower.contains("救命") || lower.contains("被困")
                || lower.contains("受伤") || lower.contains("紧急");
        if (urgent) {
            answer.append("🚨 If this is life-threatening, call now:\n")
                    .append("• Malaysia Emergency Services: ").append(EmergencyContacts.NATIONAL_EMERGENCY).append("\n")
                    .append("• UM Security Office (24h): ").append(EmergencyContacts.UM_SECURITY_DISPLAY).append("\n")
                    .append("• UM Medical Centre (UMMC): ").append(EmergencyContacts.UMMC_DISPLAY).append("\n")
                    .append("Do not walk or drive through flood water. Use the red 'SOS UM' button to share your location.");
        } else if (lower.contains("shelter") || lower.contains("evacuat") || lower.contains("where to go") || lower.contains("避难")) {
            answer.append("Based on your mobility preference (").append(mobility).append("), here are the official UM Evacuation Shelters:\n");
            for (CampusRepository.ShelterInfo s : repository.getCampusShelters()) {
                answer.append("🏛️ ").append(s.name).append(" — ").append(s.description).append("\n");
            }
            answer.append("The Map tab shows an illustrative route estimate from locally recorded hazards; verify current conditions, accessibility, and official UM guidance before moving.");
        } else if (lower.contains("route") || lower.contains("safe") || lower.contains("fcsit") || lower.contains("pantai") || lower.contains("kk12") || lower.contains("kk8")) {
            answer.append("Live Route & Hazard Assessment (Current rain: ").append(rain)
                    .append(" mm, 3h forecast: ").append(forecast3h).append(" mm):\n");
            if (activeCount == 0) {
                answer.append("No active hazards are recorded in this device's local report list. This does not confirm that routes are clear; check current conditions and official campus alerts.");
            } else {
                answer.append("There are ").append(activeCount).append(" active hazards right now:\n")
                        .append(activeLocations)
                        .append("Recommendation: Avoid Lingkungan Budi valley road; take the Perdanasiswa (KPS) elevated walkway to KK8 or DTC Shelter.");
            }
        } else if (lower.contains("sos") || lower.contains("help") || lower.contains("trapped") || lower.contains("phone") || lower.contains("contact")) {
            answer.append("🚨 Immediate Emergency Contacts:\n")
                    .append("• Malaysia Emergency Services: ").append(EmergencyContacts.NATIONAL_EMERGENCY).append("\n")
                    .append("• UM Security Office (24h): ").append(EmergencyContacts.UM_SECURITY_DISPLAY).append("\n")
                    .append("• UM Medical Centre (UMMC): ").append(EmergencyContacts.UMMC_DISPLAY)
                    .append(" | UM Clinic: ").append(EmergencyContacts.UM_CLINIC_DISPLAY).append("\n")
                    .append("• Saved Personal Emergency Contact: ")
                    .append(repository.getProfileField("emergency_name", "None"))
                    .append(" (").append(repository.getProfileField("emergency_phone", "Not set")).append(")\n")
                    .append("Use the red 'SOS UM' button at the top bar to share your live Google Maps coordinates.");
        } else {
            answer.append("Live Campus Status Summary:\n")
                    .append("• Active Unresolved Hazards in SQLite: ").append(activeCount).append("\n")
                    .append("• Current Rainfall: ").append(rain).append(" mm | Next 3h Forecast: ").append(forecast3h).append(" mm\n")
                    .append("• Mobility Mode: ").append(mobility).append("\n")
                    .append("You can ask me about 'nearest shelters', 'safe route from Pantai Gate', or 'emergency numbers'.");
        }

        answer.append("\n⚠️ Disclaimer: In any life-threatening emergency, prioritize dialing ")
                .append(EmergencyContacts.NATIONAL_EMERGENCY)
                .append(" or UM Security Office (")
                .append(EmergencyContacts.UM_SECURITY_DISPLAY).append(").");
        return answer.toString();
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\WeatherAlertDispatcher.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import android.content.Context;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Evaluates a reading and posts the notifications that are due. The Weather tab and the background worker both call
 * this, and both share the cooldown stored in {@link AlertPrefs}, so a user never gets the same alert twice.
 */
public final class WeatherAlertDispatcher {
    private WeatherAlertDispatcher() {}

    public static final class Result {
        public final int applicable;   // alerts whose threshold is exceeded
        public final int posted;       // notifications actually shown
        public final String summary;

        Result(int applicable, int posted, String summary) {
            this.applicable = applicable;
            this.posted = posted;
            this.summary = summary;
        }
    }

    /** @param demo true for simulated data: ignores the cooldown, prefixes the title, never consumes the real cooldown */
    public static Result dispatch(Context context, WeatherSnapshot snapshot, boolean demo) {
        CampusRepository repo = CampusRepository.getInstance();
        AlertPrefs prefs = new AlertPrefs(context);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        List<AlertEngine.Alert> alerts = AlertEngine.evaluate(snapshot);

        int posted = 0;
        StringBuilder names = new StringBuilder();
        for (AlertEngine.Alert a : alerts) {
            if (!demo && !AlertEngine.shouldNotify(prefs, a, now)) continue;
            String title = demo ? "[DEMO] " + a.title : a.title;
            if (repo.sendLocalAlertNotification(a.notificationId(), title, a.message)) {
                posted++;
                if (names.length() > 0) names.append(", ");
                names.append(a.type);
                if (!demo) prefs.recordNotified(a.type, a.level, now);
            }
        }

        String summary;
        if (alerts.isEmpty()) {
            summary = "No threshold exceeded";
        } else if (posted > 0) {
            summary = "Alert sent: " + names;
        } else {
            summary = "Threshold exceeded, no notification (cooldown or notifications blocked)";
        }
        return new Result(alerts.size(), posted, summary);
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\WeatherAlertScheduler.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import android.content.Context;

import androidx.work.BackoffPolicy;
import androidx.work.Constraints;
import androidx.work.Data;
import androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy;
import androidx.work.NetworkType;
import androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequest;
import androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequest;
import androidx.work.WorkManager;

import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/** Starts / stops the periodic weather check according to the user's switch. Safe to call repeatedly. */
public final class WeatherAlertScheduler {
    private WeatherAlertScheduler() {}

    static final String WORK_NAME = "um_clearflow_weather_alerts";

    /** Call on every app start and whenever the switch changes. KEEP means a running schedule is not reset. */
    public static void apply(Context context) {
        Context app = context.getApplicationContext();
        WorkManager wm = WorkManager.getInstance(app);
        if (!new AlertPrefs(app).isEnabled()) {
            wm.cancelUniqueWork(WORK_NAME);
            return;
        }
        Constraints needsNetwork = new Constraints.Builder()
                .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
                .build();
        // 15 minutes is WorkManager's minimum period
        PeriodicWorkRequest request = new PeriodicWorkRequest.Builder(WeatherAlertWorker.class, 15, TimeUnit.MINUTES)
                .setConstraints(needsNetwork)
                .setBackoffCriteria(BackoffPolicy.LINEAR, 5, TimeUnit.MINUTES)
                .build();
        wm.enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(WORK_NAME, ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP, request);
    }

    /** One immediate background run (for the "Run check now" button and for demos). */
    public static void runNow(Context context, boolean simulate) {
        OneTimeWorkRequest.Builder b = new OneTimeWorkRequest.Builder(WeatherAlertWorker.class)
                .setInputData(new Data.Builder().putBoolean(WeatherAlertWorker.KEY_SIMULATE, simulate).build());
        if (!simulate) {
            b.setConstraints(new Constraints.Builder().setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED).build());
        }
        WorkManager.getInstance(context.getApplicationContext()).enqueue(b.build());
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\WeatherAlertWorker.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import android.content.Context;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.work.Worker;
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters;

import org.json.JSONException;

import java.io.IOException;

/**
 * Periodic background check (FR 5.3). WorkManager runs it even when the app is closed (not force-stopped),
 * roughly every 15 minutes at best: the OS batches work in Doze / battery saver, so timing is approximate.
 */
public class WeatherAlertWorker extends Worker {

    public static final String KEY_SIMULATE = "simulate";
    private static final int MAX_NETWORK_RETRIES = 3;

    public WeatherAlertWorker(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull WorkerParameters params) {
        super(context, params);
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public Result doWork() {
        Context ctx = getApplicationContext();
        CampusRepository.init(ctx);   // the process may have been started just for this job
        AlertPrefs prefs = new AlertPrefs(ctx);
        boolean simulate = getInputData().getBoolean(KEY_SIMULATE, false);
        if (!simulate && !prefs.isEnabled()) {
            return Result.success();
        }

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        WeatherSnapshot snapshot;
        try {
            snapshot = simulate ? WeatherSnapshot.simulatedSquall() : WeatherService.fetch();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            prefs.recordCheck(now, "Weather service unreachable");
            return getRunAttemptCount() < MAX_NETWORK_RETRIES ? Result.retry() : Result.success();
        } catch (JSONException e) {
            prefs.recordCheck(now, "Unexpected weather response");
            return Result.success();   // retrying the same bad payload will not help
        }

        if (!simulate) {
            CampusRepository.getInstance().updateWeatherMetrics(snapshot.currentRainMm, snapshot.next3hRainMm);
        }
        WeatherAlertDispatcher.Result r = WeatherAlertDispatcher.dispatch(ctx, snapshot, simulate);
        prefs.recordCheck(now, (simulate ? "[Simulated] " : "") + r.summary);
        return Result.success();
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\WeatherService.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONException;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.URL;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

/**
 * Open-Meteo client used by BOTH the Weather tab and the background worker, so there is one parser and one set of
 * thresholds. The request uses fixed campus coordinates, never the device location.
 * Blocking: call from a background thread only.
 */
public final class WeatherService {
    private WeatherService() {}

    static final String FORECAST_URL = "https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast"
            + "?latitude=3.1245&longitude=101.6525"
            + "&current=temperature_2m,precipitation,wind_speed_10m"
            + "&hourly=precipitation,precipitation_probability"
            + "&forecast_hours=6&timezone=Asia%2FKuala_Lumpur";

    public static WeatherSnapshot fetch() throws IOException, JSONException {
        HttpURLConnection conn = (HttpURLConnection) new URL(FORECAST_URL).openConnection();
        try {
            conn.setRequestMethod("GET");
            conn.setConnectTimeout(5000);
            conn.setReadTimeout(5000);
            int code = conn.getResponseCode();
            if (code != HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) {
                throw new IOException("Open-Meteo HTTP " + code);
            }
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(
                    new InputStreamReader(conn.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
                String line;
                while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) sb.append(line);
            }
            return parse(sb.toString());
        } finally {
            conn.disconnect();
        }
    }

    /** Pure function, unit-tested on the JVM. */
    public static WeatherSnapshot parse(String json) throws JSONException {
        JSONObject root = new JSONObject(json);
        JSONObject current = root.getJSONObject("current");
        double temp = current.optDouble("temperature_2m", 28.0);
        double rain = current.optDouble("precipitation", 0.0);
        double wind = current.optDouble("wind_speed_10m", 5.0);

        JSONObject hourly = root.getJSONObject("hourly");
        JSONArray rainArr = hourly.getJSONArray("precipitation");
        JSONArray probArr = hourly.getJSONArray("precipitation_probability");

        double next3h = 0.0;
        double next6h = 0.0;
        int maxProb = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < rainArr.length(); i++) {
            double r = rainArr.optDouble(i, 0.0);
            int p = probArr.optInt(i, 0);
            if (i < 3) next3h += r;
            next6h += r;
            if (p > maxProb) maxProb = p;
        }
        return new WeatherSnapshot(temp, rain, wind, next3h, next6h, maxProb);
    }

    /** Short SOP line shown on the Weather tab. */
    public static String buildResilienceTip(double tempC, double rainMm, double next3hMm, double windKmh) {
        if (rainMm >= 5.0 || next3hMm >= 15.0) {
            return "Monsoon Squall SOP — Avoid low-lying Lingkungan Budi & Jalan Ilmu culverts; take KPS elevated covered walkway.";
        }
        if (windKmh >= 24.0) {
            return "Thunderstorm & Gust SOP — Watch for falling branches along KK12 & Pantai Gate; shelter inside concrete buildings.";
        }
        if (tempC >= 33.0) {
            return "Extreme Heat & Haze SOP — Hydrate at KPS water stations, minimize outdoor walking 12:00-15:00, wear N95 if API > 100.";
        }
        return "Clear/Moderate Weather — Report blocked storm grates early via Report tab to prevent flash ponding.";
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\src\main\java\com\um\clearflow\WeatherSnapshot.java ==========
package com.um.clearflow;

/** One reading of campus weather: current conditions plus the next 6 hours. Immutable, no Android dependencies. */
public final class WeatherSnapshot {
    public final double tempC;
    public final double currentRainMm;   // mm in the current hour
    public final double windKmh;
    public final double next3hRainMm;
    public final double next6hRainMm;
    public final int maxProbPct;         // max precipitation probability in the next 6 h

    public WeatherSnapshot(double tempC, double currentRainMm, double windKmh,
                           double next3hRainMm, double next6hRainMm, int maxProbPct) {
        this.tempC = tempC;
        this.currentRainMm = currentRainMm;
        this.windKmh = windKmh;
        this.next3hRainMm = next3hRainMm;
        this.next6hRainMm = next6hRainMm;
        this.maxProbPct = maxProbPct;
    }

    /** Demo data for the "Simulate squall" buttons. Never mixed with real readings. */
    public static WeatherSnapshot simulatedSquall() {
        return new WeatherSnapshot(25.4, 14.5, 32.0, 38.0, 55.0, 95);
    }
}

========== FILE: D:\AndroidStudioProjects\UMClearFlow\app\build.gradle.kts ==========
import java.util.Properties

plugins {
    alias(libs.plugins.android.application)
}

val localProperties = Properties()
val localPropertiesFile = rootProject.file("local.properties")
if (localPropertiesFile.exists()) {
    localPropertiesFile.inputStream().use { localProperties.load(it) }
}
val apiBaseUrl = localProperties.getProperty("API_BASE_URL", "http://10.0.2.2:8000/")
// SHA-256("clearflow-admin-v1:" + PIN). Override in local.properties (ADMIN_PIN_HASH=...) so the real PIN never ships in source.
val adminPinHash = localProperties.getProperty(
    "ADMIN_PIN_HASH", "c38a3d0d4a1fc5f2538d1445ede4008f3bf69833f13417ca107887ff3ea5fa37")

android {
    namespace = "com.um.clearflow"
    compileSdk {
        version = release(37)
    }

    defaultConfig {
        applicationId = "com.um.clearflow"
        minSdk = 26
        targetSdk = 36
        versionCode = 1
        versionName = "1.0"

        testInstrumentationRunner = "androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner"
        buildConfigField("String", "API_BASE_URL", "\"$apiBaseUrl\"")
        buildConfigField("String", "ADMIN_PIN_HASH", "\"$adminPinHash\"")
    }

    buildFeatures {
        buildConfig = true
    }

    testOptions {
        unitTests {
            isReturnDefaultValues = true
            all { it.forkEvery = 1 }
        }
    }

    buildTypes {
        release {
            optimization {
                enable = true
                packageScope = setOf("androidx.**", "kotlin.**", "kotlinx.**")
            }
        }
    }
    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_11
        targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_11
    }
}

dependencies {
    implementation(libs.activity.ktx)
    implementation(libs.appcompat)
    implementation(libs.constraintlayout)
    implementation(libs.material)
    implementation(libs.osmdroid.android)
    implementation(libs.lifecycle.viewmodel)
    implementation(libs.lifecycle.livedata)
    implementation("androidx.work:work-runtime:2.10.0")

    testImplementation(libs.junit)
    testImplementation("org.json:json:20240303")
    androidTestImplementation(libs.espresso.core)
    androidTestImplementation(libs.ext.junit)
}
